### Ranger Subclass: Beast Master

_Fight Alongside a Primal Companion_

Beast Masters form a deep, magical bond with a beast companion, one that fights alongside them as a true ally rather than a simple animal.

#### Level 3: Primal Companion

You magically summon a primal beast that becomes your companion, choosing its stat block from among Beast of the Land, Beast of the Sea, and Beast of the Sky (see below), and choosing what kind of animal it represents within that category. The beast is Friendly to you and your allies, obeys your commands, and uses your Proficiency Bonus rather than its own where applicable. If you die, your beast companion vanishes.

_In Combat._ Your beast acts during your turn. It can move and use its Reaction on its own, but the only action it takes on its own is the Dodge action, unless you take a Bonus Action to command it to take an action from its stat block or another action. Alternatively, when you take the Attack action, you can forgo one of your own attacks to let your beast use its Beast's Strike instead. If you have the Incapacitated condition, your beast can act and move on its own, taking any action of its choice, not just Dodge.

_Recovery._ If your beast companion has died within the last hour, you can use a Magic action, touch it, and expend a spell slot to return it to life with all its Hit Points restored, after a delay of 1 minute. Whenever you finish a Long Rest, you can release your companion and magically summon a different beast to take its place.

_Shared Traits._ All three beast options share the following: their Armor Class equals 13 plus your Wisdom modifier; their Beast's Strike uses your spellcasting ability modifier for its attack bonus; and their Primal Bond trait adds your Proficiency Bonus to any ability check or saving throw the beast makes.

**Beast of the Land.** Hit Points equal 5 plus five times your Ranger level. Speed 40 feet, Climb Speed 40 feet. Its Beast's Strike has a 5-foot reach and deals 1d8 plus 2 plus your Wisdom modifier damage of a type you choose (Bludgeoning, Piercing, or Slashing) when you choose the beast. If the beast moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, it deals an extra 1d6 of that damage type, and the target falls Prone if it's Large or smaller.

**Beast of the Sea.** Hit Points equal 5 plus five times your Ranger level. Speed 5 feet, Swim Speed 60 feet. It can breathe air and water. Its Beast's Strike deals 1d6 plus 2 plus your Wisdom modifier damage (Bludgeoning or Piercing, chosen when you choose the beast), and the target has the Grappled condition (escape DC equals your spell save DC).

**Beast of the Sky.** Hit Points equal 4 plus four times your Ranger level. Speed 10 feet, Fly Speed 60 feet. It doesn't provoke Opportunity Attacks when it flies out of a creature's reach. Its Beast's Strike deals 1d4 plus 3 plus your Wisdom modifier Slashing damage.

#### Level 7: Exceptional Training

When you take a Bonus Action to command your beast, it can also take the Dash, Disengage, Dodge, or Help action as its own Bonus Action. In addition, whenever your beast hits with its Beast's Strike and deals damage, it can deal Force damage instead of the attack's normal damage type.

#### Level 11: Bestial Fury

When you command your beast to use its Beast's Strike, it can attack twice instead of once. In addition, the first time each turn your beast hits a creature affected by your [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hunter's Mark|Hunter's Mark]] spell, the beast deals extra Force damage to that creature equal to [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hunter's Mark|Hunter's Mark]]'s bonus damage.

#### Level 15: Share Spells

When you cast a spell that targets only yourself, you can also affect your beast companion with the spell if it's within 30 feet of you when you cast it.

### Ranger Subclass: Fey Wanderer

_Blend Martial Skill with Fey Trickery_

Fey Wanderers have been touched by the Feywild, blending the ranger's martial skill with fey glamour and guile, equally at home ambushing a foe's mind as ambushing its body.

#### Level 3: Dreadful Strikes

Once per turn when you hit a creature with a weapon, you can deal an extra 1d4 Psychic damage to the target. This damage increases to 1d6 when you reach Ranger level 11.

#### Level 3: Otherworldly Glamour

You gain proficiency in one of the following skills of your choice: Deception, Performance, or Persuasion. In addition, whenever you make a Charisma check, you can add your Wisdom modifier (minimum of +1) to the check, in addition to your Charisma modifier.

#### Level 3: Fey Wanderer Spells

You always have certain spells prepared. When you reach a Ranger level specified in the Fey Wanderer Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spell prepared.

**Fey Wanderer Spells**

| Ranger Level | Prepared Spell |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | Charm Person |
| 5 | Misty Step |
| 9 | Summon Fey |
| 13 | Dimension Door |
| 17 | Mislead |

#### Level 7: Beguiling Twist

You have Advantage on saving throws you make to avoid or end the Charmed or Frightened condition. In addition, when you or a creature you can see within 120 feet of yourself succeeds on a saving throw against being Charmed or Frightened, or is immune to either condition, you can take a Reaction to force a different creature you can see within 120 feet of yourself to make a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, that creature has the Charmed or Frightened condition (your choice) for 1 minute.

#### Level 11: Fey Reinforcements

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Fey|Summon Fey]] without Material components, and once per Long Rest you can cast it without expending a spell slot. When you cast the spell in this way, you can modify it so that the summoned fey's spirit form lasts for 1 minute without requiring your Concentration.

#### Level 15: Misty Wanderer

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Misty Step|Misty Step]] without expending a spell slot a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest. When you cast the spell in this way, you can bring along one willing creature within 5 feet of yourself to the destination space.

### Ranger Subclass: Gloom Stalker

_Ambush Threats from the Darkness_

Gloom Stalkers specialize in ambushing threats that lurk in darkness and the Underdark, built to dominate the opening moments of a fight before their enemies can react.

#### Level 3: Dread Ambusher

You gain the following benefits.

_Ambusher's Leap._ At the start of the first turn of combat, your Speed increases by 10 feet until the end of that turn.

_Dreadful Strike._ Once per turn when you hit a creature with a weapon, you can deal an extra 2d6 Psychic damage to the target. You can use this benefit a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

_Initiative Bonus._ You add your Wisdom modifier, in addition to your Dexterity modifier, to Initiative rolls.

#### Level 3: Gloom Stalker Spells

You always have certain spells prepared. When you reach a Ranger level specified in the Gloom Stalker Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spell prepared.

**Gloom Stalker Spells**

| Ranger Level | Prepared Spell |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | Disguise Self |
| 5 | Rope Trick |
| 9 | Fear |
| 13 | Greater Invisibility |
| 17 | Seeming |

#### Level 3: Umbral Sight

You have Darkvision with a range of 60 feet. If you already have Darkvision, its range increases by 60 feet instead. While you're entirely in an area of Darkness, you're Invisible to any creature that relies on Darkvision to see you in that Darkness.

#### Level 7: Iron Mind

You gain proficiency in Wisdom saving throws. If you already have this proficiency, you instead gain proficiency in Intelligence or Charisma saving throws (your choice).

#### Level 11: Stalker's Flurry

Your Dreadful Strike damage increases to 2d8. In addition, whenever you deal this extra damage, you can choose one of the following effects.

_Sudden Strike._ You make another attack with the same weapon against a second creature that is within 5 feet of the original target and within the weapon's range.

_Mass Fear._ The original target and every creature within 10 feet of it must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC or have the Frightened condition until the start of your next turn.

#### Level 15: Shadowy Dodge

When a creature makes an attack roll against you, you can take a Reaction to impose Disadvantage on that roll. Whether the attack hits or misses, you can then teleport up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space you can see.

### Ranger Subclass: Hunter

_Protect Nature and People from Destruction_

You stalk prey in the wilds and elsewhere, using your abilities as a Hunter to protect nature and people everywhere from forces that would destroy them.

#### Level 3: Hunter's Lore

You can call on the forces of nature to reveal certain strengths and weaknesses of your prey. While a creature is marked by your [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hunter's Mark|Hunter's Mark]], you know whether that creature has any Immunities, Resistances, or Vulnerabilities, and if the creature has any, you know what they are.

#### Level 3: Hunter's Prey

You gain one of the following feature options of your choice. Whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest, you can replace the chosen option with the other one.

_Colossus Slayer._ Your tenacity can wear down even the most resilient foes. When you hit a creature with a weapon, the weapon deals an extra 1d8 damage to the target if it's missing any of its Hit Points. You can deal this extra damage only once per turn.

_Horde Breaker._ Once on each of your turns when you make an attack with a weapon, you can make another attack with the same weapon against a different creature that is within 5 feet of the original target, that is within the weapon's range, and that you haven't attacked this turn.

#### Level 7: Defensive Tactics

You gain one of the following feature options of your choice. Whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest, you can replace the chosen option with the other one.

_Escape the Horde._ Opportunity Attacks have Disadvantage against you.

_Multiattack Defense._ When a creature hits you with an attack roll, that creature has Disadvantage on all other attack rolls against you this turn.

#### Level 11: Superior Hunter's Prey

Once per turn when you deal damage to a creature marked by your [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hunter's Mark|Hunter's Mark]], you can also deal that spell's extra damage to a different creature that you can see within 30 feet of the first creature.

#### Level 15: Superior Hunter's Defense

When you take damage, you can take a Reaction to give yourself Resistance to that damage and any other damage of the same type until the end of the current turn.

### Ranger Subclass: Horizon Walker

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Ranger subclasses (Beast Master/Fey Wanderer/Gloom Stalker/Hunter)—kept here as bonus content._

_Guard the Boundaries between Planes_

Horizon Walkers guard the world against threats that slip through planar rifts, gaining the ability to sense portals and step briefly into the Ethereal Plane.

#### Level 3: Detect Portal

As an action, you can sense the distance and direction to the nearest planar portal within 1 mile of yourself. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

#### Level 3: Horizon Walker Spells

You always have certain spells prepared. When you reach a Ranger level specified in the Horizon Walker Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Horizon Walker Spells**

| Ranger Level | Prepared Spell |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | Protection from Evil and Good |
| 5 | Misty Step |
| 9 | Haste |
| 13 | Banishment |
| 17 | Teleportation Circle |

#### Level 3: Planar Warrior

As a Bonus Action, choose a creature you can see within 30 feet of yourself. The next time you hit that creature with a weapon attack before the end of the current turn, all the attack's damage becomes Force damage, and the target takes an extra 1d8 Force damage (2d8 starting at level 11). You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 7: Ethereal Step

As a Bonus Action, you can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Etherealness|Etherealness]] without expending a spell slot, but the spell ends at the end of the current turn if cast this way. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

#### Level 11: Distant Strike

When you take the Attack action, you can teleport up to 10 feet to an unoccupied space you can see before each attack included in that action. If you attack at least two different creatures with the action, you can make one additional attack with it.

#### Level 15: Spectral Defense

When you take damage from an attack you can see, you can take a Reaction to give yourself Resistance to that attack's damage.

### Ranger Subclass: Monster Slayer

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Ranger subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Hunt Down Creatures of Dark Magic_

Monster Slayers dedicate themselves to hunting down creatures of dark magic—undead, fiends, aberrations, and other supernatural threats—before they can spread their corruption further.

#### Level 3: Hunter's Sense

As an action, choose a creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself; you learn whether it has any damage Immunities, Resistances, or Vulnerabilities, and if so, what they are. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 3: Monster Slayer Spells

You always have certain spells prepared. When you reach a Ranger level specified in the Monster Slayer Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Monster Slayer Spells**

| Ranger Level | Prepared Spell |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | Protection from Evil and Good |
| 5 | Zone of Truth |
| 9 | Magic Circle |
| 13 | Banishment |
| 17 | Hold Monster |

#### Level 3: Slayer's Prey

As a Bonus Action, designate one creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself as your quarry. Once per turn, the first time you hit your quarry with a weapon attack, you deal an extra 1d6 damage to it. This effect lasts until you finish a Short or Long Rest or use this feature on a new target.

#### Level 7: Supernatural Defense

Whenever your quarry forces you to make a saving throw, or whenever you make an ability check to escape a Grapple it has on you, add 1d6 to the roll.

#### Level 11: Magic-User's Nemesis

As a Reaction when you see a creature within 60 feet of yourself casting a spell or teleporting or shifting between planes of existence, you can force it to make a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, the spell or other effect fails and is wasted. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

#### Level 15: Slayer's Counter

If your quarry forces you to make a saving throw, you can take a Reaction to make one weapon attack against it as part of the Reaction; this attack occurs before the saving throw, and if it hits, you automatically succeed on the saving throw in addition to the attack's normal effects.

### Ranger Subclass: Swarmkeeper

_Expansion subclass — Tasha's Cauldron of Everything (2020). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Ranger subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Command a Swarm of Nature Spirits_

Swarmkeepers form a bond with a swarm of small nature spirits—leaves, insects, or tiny fey—which flit around them and lend their strength in battle.

#### Level 3: Gathered Swarm

Once on each of your turns, immediately after you hit a creature with an attack, your swarm can do one of the following: deal an extra 1d6 damage of a type you chose when you gained this feature (this die increases to 1d8 at level 11); force the target to make a Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 15 feet horizontally toward you; or move yourself up to 5 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

#### Level 3: Swarmkeeper Magic

You learn the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Hand|Mage Hand]] cantrip; your spectral hand can instead be your swarm, can be invisible, and doesn't need to be seen to be commanded within 30 feet of yourself. You always have certain spells prepared. When you reach a Ranger level specified in the Swarmkeeper Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Swarmkeeper Spells**

| Ranger Level | Prepared Spell |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | Faerie Fire |
| 5 | Web |
| 9 | Gaseous Form |
| 13 | Arcane Eye |
| 17 | Insect Plague |

#### Level 7: Writhing Tide

As a Bonus Action, you gain a Fly Speed of 10 feet and the ability to hover for 1 minute or until you have the Incapacitated condition. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 11: Mighty Swarm

Your Gathered Swarm feature improves. Its damage die becomes 1d8; on the pull option, a target that fails its save also has the Prone condition; and the self-move option now gives you Half Cover until the start of your next turn.

#### Level 15: Swarming Dispersal

When you take damage, you can take a Reaction to give yourself Resistance to that damage and teleport up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space you can see, as your swarm disperses and reforms around you. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

## Rogue

**Core Rogue Traits**

| Col 1 | Col 2 |
| --- | --- |
| Primary Ability | Dexterity |
| Hit Point Die | D8 per Rogue level |
| Saving Throw Proficiencies | Dexterity and Intelligence |
| Skill Proficiencies | Choose 4: Acrobatics, Athletics, Deception, Insight, Intimidation, Investigation, Perception, Persuasion, Sleight of Hand, or Stealth |
| Weapon Proficiencies | Simple weapons and Martial weapons that have the Finesse or Light property |
| Tool Proficiencies | Thieves' Tools |
| Armor Training | Light armor |
| Starting Equipment | Choose A or B: (A) Leather Armor, 2 Daggers, Shortsword, Shortbow, 20 Arrows, Quiver, Thieves' Tools, Burglar's Pack, and 8 GP; or (B) 100 GP |

### Becoming a Rogue …

#### As a Level 1 Character

- Gain all the traits in the Core Rogue Traits table.
- Gain the Rogue's level 1 features, which are listed in the Rogue Features table.

#### As a Multiclass Character

- Gain the following traits from the Core Rogue Traits table: Hit Point Die, proficiency in one skill of your choice from the Rogue's skill list, proficiency with Thieves' Tools, and training with Light armor.
- Gain the Rogue's level 1 features, which are listed in the Rogue Features table.

### Rogue Class Features

As a Rogue, you gain the following class features when you reach the specified Rogue levels. These features are listed in the Rogue Features table.

**Rogue Features**

| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Class Features | Sneak Attack |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | +2 | Expertise, Sneak Attack, Thieves' Cant, Weapon Mastery | 1d6 |
| 2 | +2 | Cunning Action | 1d6 |
| 3 | +2 | Rogue Subclass, Steady Aim | 2d6 |
| 4 | +2 | Ability Score Improvement | 2d6 |
| 5 | +3 | Cunning Strike, Uncanny Dodge | 3d6 |
| 6 | +3 | Expertise | 3d6 |
| 7 | +3 | Evasion, Reliable Talent | 4d6 |
| 8 | +3 | Ability Score Improvement | 4d6 |
| 9 | +4 | Subclass feature | 5d6 |
| 10 | +4 | Ability Score Improvement | 5d6 |
| 11 | +4 | Improved Cunning Strike | 6d6 |
| 12 | +4 | Ability Score Improvement | 6d6 |
| 13 | +5 | Subclass feature | 7d6 |
| 14 | +5 | Devious Strikes | 7d6 |
| 15 | +5 | Slippery Mind | 8d6 |
| 16 | +5 | Ability Score Improvement | 8d6 |
| 17 | +6 | Subclass feature | 9d6 |
| 18 | +6 | Elusive | 9d6 |
| 19 | +6 | Epic Boon | 10d6 |
| 20 | +6 | Stroke of Luck | 10d6 |

#### Level 1: Expertise

You gain Expertise in two of your skill proficiencies of your choice. Sleight of Hand and Stealth are recommended if you have proficiency in them.

At Rogue level 6, you gain Expertise in two more of your skill proficiencies of your choice.

#### Level 1: Sneak Attack

You know how to strike subtly and exploit a foe's distraction. Once per turn, you can deal an extra 1d6 damage to one creature you hit with an attack roll if you have Advantage on the roll and the attack uses a Finesse or a Ranged weapon. The extra damage's type is the same as the weapon's type.

You don't need Advantage on the attack roll if at least one of your allies is within 5 feet of the target, the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition, and you don't have Disadvantage on the attack roll. The extra damage increases as you gain Rogue levels, as shown in the Sneak Attack column of the Rogue Features table.

#### Level 1: Thieves' Cant

You picked up various languages in the communities where you plied your roguish talents. You know Thieves' Cant and one other language of your choice, which you choose from the language tables in "Character Creation."

#### Level 1: Weapon Mastery

Your training with weapons allows you to use the mastery properties of two kinds of weapons of your choice with which you have proficiency, such as Daggers and Shortbows.

Whenever you finish a Long Rest, you can change the kinds of weapons you chose. For example, you could switch to using the mastery properties of Scimitars and Shortswords.

#### Level 2: Cunning Action

Your quick thinking and agility allow you to move and act quickly. On your turn, you can take one of the following actions as a Bonus Action: Dash, Disengage, or Hide.

#### Level 3: Rogue Subclass

You gain a Rogue subclass of your choice. The Thief subclass is detailed after this class's description. A subclass is a specialization that grants you features at certain Rogue levels. For the rest of your career, you gain each of your subclass's features that are of your Rogue level or lower.

#### Level 3: Steady Aim

As a Bonus Action, you give yourself Advantage on your next attack roll on the current turn. You can use this feature only if you haven't moved during this turn, and after you use it, your Speed is 0 until the end of the current turn.

#### Level 4: Ability Score Improvement

You gain the Ability Score Improvement feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. You gain this feature again at Rogue levels 8, 10, 12, and 16.

#### Level 5: Cunning Strike

You've developed cunning ways to use your Sneak Attack. When you deal Sneak Attack damage, you can add one of the following Cunning Strike effects. Each effect has a die cost, which is the number of Sneak Attack damage dice you must forgo to add the effect. You remove the die before rolling, and the effect occurs immediately after the attack's damage is dealt. For example, if you add the Poison effect, remove 1d6 from the Sneak Attack's damage before rolling.

If a Cunning Strike effect requires a saving throw, the DC equals 8 plus your Dexterity modifier and Proficiency Bonus.

_Poison (Cost: 1d6)._ You add a toxin to your strike, forcing the target to make a Constitution saving throw. On a failed save, the target has the Poisoned condition for 1 minute. At the end of each of its turns, the Poisoned target repeats the save, ending the effect on itself on a success.

To use this effect, you must have a Poisoner's Kit on your person.

_Trip (Cost: 1d6)._ If the target is Large or smaller, it must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw or have the Prone condition.

_Withdraw (Cost: 1d6)._ Immediately after the attack, you move up to half your Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

#### Level 5: Uncanny Dodge

When an attacker that you can see hits you with an attack roll, you can take a Reaction to halve the attack's damage against you (round down).

#### Level 7: Evasion

You can nimbly dodge out of the way of certain dangers. When you're subjected to an effect that allows you to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, you instead take no damage if you succeed on the saving throw and only half damage if you fail. You can't use this feature if you have the Incapacitated condition.

#### Level 7: Reliable Talent

Whenever you make an ability check that uses one of your skill or tool proficiencies, you can treat a d20 roll of 9 or lower as a 10.

#### Level 11: Improved Cunning Strike

You can use up to two Cunning Strike effects when you deal Sneak Attack damage, paying the die cost for each effect.

#### Level 14: Devious Strikes

You've practiced new ways to use your Sneak Attack deviously. The following effects are now among your Cunning Strike options.

_Daze (Cost: 2d6)._ The target must succeed on a Constitution saving throw, or on its next turn, it can do only one of the following: move or take an action or a Bonus Action.

_Knock Out (Cost: 6d6)._ The target must succeed on a Constitution saving throw, or it has the Unconscious condition for 1 minute or until it takes any damage. The Unconscious target repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.

_Obscure (Cost: 3d6)._ The target must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw, or it has the Blinded condition until the end of its next turn.

#### Level 15: Slippery Mind

Your cunning mind is exceptionally difficult to control. You gain proficiency in Wisdom and Charisma saving throws.

#### Level 18: Elusive

You're so evasive that attackers rarely gain the upper hand against you. No attack roll can have Advantage against you unless you have the Incapacitated condition.

#### Level 19: Epic Boon

You gain an Epic Boon feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. Boon of the Night Spirit is recommended.

#### Level 20: Stroke of Luck

You have a marvelous knack for succeeding when you need to. If you fail a D20 Test, you can turn the roll into a 20.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

### Rogue Subclass: Arcane Trickster

_Blend Roguery with Wizardry_

Arcane Tricksters supplement stealth and skulduggery with a slice of wizardry, using illusion and enchantment magic—and an animated spectral hand—to make heists and cons even smoother.

#### Level 3: Spellcasting

You augment your roguish talents with spells drawn from the Wizard spell list. Intelligence is your spellcasting ability, and you use an Arcane Focus or a Component Pouch as a Spellcasting Focus.

_Cantrips._ You know three cantrips: [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Hand|Mage Hand]] and two other Wizard cantrips of your choice. You can replace one of your non–[[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Hand|Mage Hand]] cantrips whenever you gain a Rogue level, and you learn an additional Wizard cantrip of your choice at level 10.

_Prepared Spells and Spell Slots._ You prepare Wizard spells and gain spell slots according to the Arcane Trickster Spellcasting table.

**Arcane Trickster Spellcasting**

| Rogue Level | Prepared Spells | 1st | 2nd | 3rd | 4th |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 3 | 3 | 2 | — | — | — |
| 4 | 4 | 3 | — | — | — |
| 5 | 4 | 3 | — | — | — |
| 6 | 4 | 3 | — | — | — |
| 7 | 5 | 4 | 2 | — | — |
| 8 | 6 | 4 | 2 | — | — |
| 9 | 6 | 4 | 2 | — | — |
| 10 | 7 | 4 | 3 | — | — |
| 11 | 8 | 4 | 3 | — | — |
| 12 | 8 | 4 | 3 | — | — |
| 13 | 9 | 4 | 3 | 2 | — |
| 14 | 10 | 4 | 3 | 2 | — |
| 15 | 10 | 4 | 3 | 2 | — |
| 16 | 11 | 4 | 3 | 3 | — |
| 17 | 11 | 4 | 3 | 3 | — |
| 18 | 11 | 4 | 3 | 3 | — |
| 19 | 12 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 1 |
| 20 | 13 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 1 |

#### Level 3: Mage Hand Legerdemain

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Hand|Mage Hand]] as a Bonus Action, and you can make its spectral hand invisible. As a Bonus Action, you can control the hand to take any action the spell allows, including making a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) check to lift a purse, plant an item, or pick a lock or a pocket, all from a distance.

#### Level 9: Magical Ambush

If you're Hidden from a creature when you cast a spell on it, that creature has Disadvantage on any saving throw it makes against the spell this turn.

#### Level 13: Versatile Trickster

You can use the Trip option of your Cunning Strike feature against a creature within 5 feet of your [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Hand|Mage Hand]], in addition to creatures within your own reach.

#### Level 17: Spell Thief

When a creature casts a spell that targets you, you can take a Reaction to force it to make an Intelligence saving throw against the spell's own save DC. On a failed save, the spell has no effect on you, and you steal its magic: you can cast that spell once within the next 8 hours, provided it's a spell you could normally prepare or cast and is level 1 or higher, without expending a spell slot. The original caster can't cast that spell again until your stolen casting is used or 8 hours pass.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Rogue Subclass: Assassin

_Deliver the Perfect Opening Strike_

Assassins are rogues devoted to ending a fight in its opening moments, relying on poison, disguise, and a single devastating first blow rather than sustained combat.

#### Level 3: Assassinate

You have Advantage on Initiative rolls. In addition, during the first round of combat, you have Advantage on attack rolls against any creature that hasn't yet taken a turn. If you hit a creature with a Sneak Attack during the first round of combat, that target takes extra damage of the weapon's damage type equal to your Rogue level.

#### Level 3: Assassin's Tools

You gain proficiency with the Disguise Kit and the Poisoner's Kit.

#### Level 9: Infiltration Expertise

Given at least 1 hour to study a person's speech, handwriting, and mannerisms—in person, or via recordings or written notes—you can create a convincing disguise of that person, using the Disguise Kit. In addition, when you use your Steady Aim feature, your Speed is no longer reduced to 0.

#### Level 13: Envenom Weapons

When you use the Poison option of your Cunning Strike feature and the target fails its saving throw, the target takes an extra 2d6 Poison damage, and this extra damage ignores Resistance to Poison damage.

#### Level 17: Death Strike

When you hit a creature with a Sneak Attack during the first round of combat, that target must succeed on a Constitution saving throw (DC 8 plus your Dexterity modifier and Proficiency Bonus) or the damage of your attack is doubled against it.

### Rogue Subclass: Soulknife

_Manifest Blades of Psychic Energy_

Soulknives have discovered a latent psionic gift, manifesting blades of pure psychic energy in place of steel, alongside telepathy and short-range teleportation.

#### Level 3: Psionic Power

You can harness psionic energy, gaining the following benefits.

_Psionic Energy Dice._ You have a number of Psionic Energy Dice equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus. These dice are d6s, and they turn into d8s at Rogue level 5, d10s at level 11, and d12s at level 17. You regain one expended die when you finish a Short Rest, and you regain all expended dice when you finish a Long Rest.

_Psi-Bolstered Knack._ When you fail an ability check using a skill or tool with which you have proficiency, you can roll one Psionic Energy Die and add the number rolled to the check. The die is expended only if doing so causes the check to succeed.

_Psychic Whispers._ As a Magic action, you choose a number of creatures, up to your Proficiency Bonus, that you can see or that you've seen before, within 1 mile of yourself. You and the chosen creatures can communicate telepathically for a number of hours equal to a roll of one Psionic Energy Die. The first time you use this feature after finishing a Long Rest, it doesn't expend a die.

#### Level 3: Psychic Blades

As part of the Attack action, or as an Opportunity Attack, you can manifest a shimmering Psychic Blade in an empty hand and attack with it. The blade has the Finesse and Thrown (range 60/120 feet) properties, and it deals 1d6 Psychic damage plus your attack's ability modifier. It also has the Vex mastery property.

If your other hand is free, you can make an additional attack with a second Psychic Blade as a Bonus Action; this attack deals 1d4 Psychic damage plus your ability modifier, following the normal rules for two-weapon attacks.

#### Level 9: Soul Blades

You gain the following benefits.

_Homing Strikes._ When you miss with an attack using your Psychic Blades, you can expend one Psionic Energy Die to reroll the attack roll.

_Psychic Teleportation._ As a Bonus Action, you can expend one Psionic Energy Die and teleport up to a number of feet equal to 10 times the number rolled, to an unoccupied space you can see.

#### Level 13: Psychic Veil

As a Magic action, you can expend one Psionic Energy Die to turn Invisible for up to 1 hour, or until you deal damage or force a creature to make a saving throw.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend a Psionic Energy Die (no action required) to use it again.

#### Level 17: Rend Mind

When you hit a creature with a Sneak Attack, you can force it to make a Wisdom saving throw (DC 8 plus your Dexterity modifier and Proficiency Bonus). On a failed save, the target has the Stunned condition for 1 minute, repeating the save at the end of each of its turns.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend 3 Psionic Energy Dice at once (no action required) to use it again.

### Rogue Subclass: Thief

_Hunt for Treasure as a Classic Adventurer_

A mix of burglar, treasure hunter, and explorer, you are the epitome of an adventurer. In addition to improving your agility and stealth, you gain abilities useful for delving into ruins and getting maximum benefit from the magic items you find there.

#### Level 3: Fast Hands

As a Bonus Action, you can do one of the following.

_Sleight of Hand._ Make a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) check to pick a lock or disarm a trap with Thieves' Tools or to pick a pocket.

_Use an Object._ Take the Utilize action, or take the Magic action to use a magic item that requires that action.

#### Level 3: Second-Story Work

You've trained to get into especially hard-to-reach places, granting you these benefits.

_Climber._ You gain a Climb Speed equal to your Speed.

_Jumper._ You can determine your jump distance using your Dexterity rather than your Strength.

#### Level 9: Supreme Sneak

You gain the following Cunning Strike option.

_Stealth Attack (Cost: 1d6)._ If you have the Hide action's Invisible condition, this attack doesn't end that condition on you if you end the turn behind Three-Quarters Cover or Total Cover.

#### Level 13: Use Magic Device

You've learned how to maximize use of magic items, granting you the following benefits.

_Attunement._ You can attune to up to four magic items at once.

_Charges._ Whenever you use a magic item property that expends charges, roll 1d6. On a roll of 6, you use the property without expending the charges.

_Scrolls._ You can use any [[z_5.5e Compendium/Magic Items#Spell Scroll|Spell Scroll]], using Intelligence as your spellcasting ability for the spell. If the spell is a cantrip or a level 1 spell, you can cast it reliably. If the scroll contains a higher-level spell, you must first succeed on an Intelligence (Arcana) check (DC 10 plus the spell's level). On a successful check, you cast the spell from the scroll. On a failed check, the scroll disintegrates.

#### Level 17: Thief's Reflexes

You are adept at laying ambushes and quickly escaping danger. You can take two turns during the first round of any combat. You take your first turn at your normal Initiative and your second turn at your Initiative minus 10.

### Rogue Subclass: Mastermind

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Rogue subclasses (Arcane Trickster/Assassin/Soulknife/Thief)—kept here as bonus content._

_Direct Allies from the Shadows_

Masterminds prefer to let others take the front line, directing their allies' actions from a step removed and manipulating both friend and foe alike toward their own ends.

#### Level 3: Master of Intrigue

You gain proficiency with the Disguise Kit, the Forgery Kit, and one type of Gaming Set of your choice. You also learn two languages of your choice. After hearing a creature speak for at least 1 minute, you can mimic its speech patterns and accent, provided you know its language; a successful Charisma (Deception) check contested by the listener's passive Wisdom (Insight) score is needed to fool a listener into thinking you're a native speaker.

#### Level 3: Master of Tactics

You can use the Help action as a Bonus Action. In addition, when you use the Help action to aid an ally's attack, the target of that attack can be within 30 feet of you, rather than requiring you to be within 5 feet of the target, provided the target can see or hear you.

#### Level 9: Insightful Manipulator

As an action, you can spend at least 1 minute observing or interacting with a creature outside combat to learn whether it is your equal, superior, or inferior in two of the following: Intelligence, Wisdom, Charisma, and class levels (if any).

#### Level 13: Misdirection

If a creature within 5 feet of yourself is granting you Cover against an attack, you can take a Reaction to cause the attack to instead target that creature.

#### Level 17: Soul of Deceit

Your thoughts can't be read by telepathy unless you allow it. You can present false thoughts to a telepathic probe by succeeding on a Charisma (Deception) check contested by the prober's Insight. Magic can't determine whether you're lying, and you can even lie while under a magical compulsion to tell only the truth, resisting such effects at will.

### Rogue Subclass: Phantom

_Expansion subclass — Tasha's Cauldron of Everything (2020). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Rogue subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Bear the Whispers of the Dead_

Phantoms have brushed against death itself and returned marked by it, carrying whispers of the departed and drawing on their power in battle.

#### Level 3: Whispers of the Dead

Whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest, you can choose one skill proficiency or one tool proficiency granted by a ghostly whisper, from the following options: Arcana, Athletics, Intimidation, Investigation, Medicine, Nature, or Religion skill proficiency, proficiency with the Stealth skill, or proficiency with one type of Artisan's Tools or Gaming Set. You gain that proficiency until you next choose a different one with this feature.

#### Level 3: Wails from the Grave

Whenever you deal your Sneak Attack damage, you can also cause a second creature within 30 feet of the original target to take Necrotic damage equal to half your Sneak Attack damage dice for your level (round the number of dice up), rolled separately. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 9: Tokens of the Departed

When a creature dies within 30 feet of yourself, you can take a Reaction to create a Tiny soul trinket in the space where it died. You can hold a number of these trinkets equal to your Proficiency Bonus. While holding at least one trinket, you have Advantage on Death Saving Throws and on Constitution saving throws to maintain Concentration. You can destroy one trinket to use Wails from the Grave without expending a use of it, or destroy one to ask the departed spirit one question, which it answers truthfully to the best of its knowledge (though it need not be helpful), in a language it knew in life.

#### Level 13: Ghost Walk

As a Bonus Action, you assume a spectral, ghostlike form for 10 minutes or until you end it early (a Bonus Action). While in this form, you have a Fly Speed of 10 feet and can hover; attack rolls against you have Disadvantage; and you can move through creatures and objects as though they were Difficult Terrain, though you take 1d10 Force damage if you end your turn inside an object or a creature. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you destroy a soul trinket as part of the Bonus Action to activate it again.

#### Level 17: Death's Friend

When you use Wails from the Grave, its Necrotic damage now applies to both the original Sneak Attack target and the second creature. In addition, if you have no soul trinkets when you finish a Long Rest, you create one automatically.

### Rogue Subclass: Scout

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Rogue subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Range Ahead as a Skilled Wilderness Scout_

Scouts combine a Rogue's stealth with a Ranger's skill at wilderness survival, specializing in reconnaissance, ambush, and rapid movement across difficult terrain.

#### Level 3: Skirmisher

When a creature you can see moves within 5 feet of yourself, you can take a Reaction to move up to half your Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks from that creature.

#### Level 3: Survivalist

You gain proficiency in the Nature and Survival skills, if you don't already have them, and your Proficiency Bonus is doubled for any ability check you make using either skill.

#### Level 9: Superior Mobility

Your Speed increases by 10 feet. If you have a Climb Speed or a Swim Speed, it also increases by 10 feet.

#### Level 13: Ambush Master

You have Advantage on Initiative rolls. In addition, the first creature you hit during the first turn of combat becomes easier for your whole party to hit: attack rolls against that target have Advantage until the start of your next turn.

#### Level 17: Sudden Strike

When you take the Attack action on your turn, you can make one additional attack as part of that action. This extra attack can benefit from your Sneak Attack even if you've already applied Sneak Attack this turn, though you still can't apply Sneak Attack to more than one attack against the same target in a single turn.

### Rogue Subclass: Swashbuckler

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Rogue subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Duel with Flair and Audacity_

Swashbucklers combine swordplay with charm, fighting one-on-one duels with a flourish and using their audacity to keep multiple foes off balance.

#### Level 3: Fancy Footwork

During your turn, if you make a melee attack against a creature, that creature can't make Opportunity Attacks against you for the rest of your turn, whether or not your attack hits.

#### Level 3: Rakish Audacity

You gain a bonus to your Initiative rolls equal to your Charisma modifier. In addition, you don't need Advantage on the attack roll to use your Sneak Attack if no creature other than your target is within 5 feet of you and you don't have Disadvantage on the attack roll; you need only your target to be within 5 feet of you.

#### Level 9: Panache

As an action, you can make a Charisma (Persuasion) check contested by one creature's Wisdom (Insight) check; the target must be able to hear you, and you must share a language with it. If it's hostile and you succeed, it has Disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than you, and it can't make Opportunity Attacks against anyone but you, for 1 minute; this ends early if one of your companions attacks it or affects it with a harmful spell, or if you move more than 60 feet away from it. If it isn't hostile and you succeed, it has the Charmed condition toward you for 1 minute and regards you as a friendly acquaintance; this ends immediately if you or a companion does anything harmful to it.

#### Level 13: Elegant Maneuver

As a Bonus Action, you gain Advantage on the next Dexterity (Acrobatics) check or Strength (Athletics) check you make during the current turn.

#### Level 17: Master Duelist

If you miss with an attack roll, you can roll it again with Advantage. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

## Sorcerer

**Core Sorcerer Traits**

| Col 1 | Col 2 |
| --- | --- |
| Primary Ability | Charisma |
| Hit Point Die | D6 per Sorcerer level |
| Saving Throw Proficiencies | Constitution and Charisma |
| Skill Proficiencies | Choose 2: Arcana, Deception, Insight, Intimidation, Persuasion, or Religion |
| Weapon Proficiencies | Simple weapons |
| Armor Training | None |
| Starting Equipment | Choose A or B: (A) Spear, 2 Daggers, Arcane Focus (crystal), Dungeoneer's Pack, and 28 GP; or (B) 50 GP |

### Becoming a Sorcerer …

#### As a Level 1 Character

- Gain all the traits in the Core Sorcerer Traits table.
- Gain the Sorcerer's level 1 features, which are listed in the Sorcerer Features table.

#### As a Multiclass Character

- Gain the Hit Point Die from the Core Sorcerer Traits table.
- Gain the Sorcerer's level 1 features, which are listed in the Sorcerer Features table. See the multiclassing rules in "Character Creation" to determine your available spell slots.

### Sorcerer Class Features

As a Sorcerer, you gain the following class features when you reach the specified Sorcerer levels. These features are listed in the Sorcerer Features table.

**Sorcerer Features**

| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Class Features | Sorcery Points | Cantrips | Prepared Spells | Lvl 1 Slots | Lvl 2 Slots | Lvl 3 Slots | Lvl 4 Slots | Lvl 5 Slots | Lvl 6 Slots | Lvl 7 Slots | Lvl 8 Slots | Lvl 9 Slots |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | +2 | Spellcasting, Innate Sorcery | — | 4 | 2 | 2 | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — |
| 2 | +2 | Font of Magic, Metamagic | 2 | 4 | 4 | 3 | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — |
| 3 | +2 | Sorcerer Subclass | 3 | 4 | 6 | 4 | 2 | — | — | — | — | — | — | — |
| 4 | +2 | Ability Score Improvement | 4 | 5 | 7 | 4 | 3 | — | — | — | — | — | — | — |
| 5 | +3 | Sorcerous Restoration | 5 | 5 | 9 | 4 | 3 | 2 | — | — | — | — | — | — |
| 6 | +3 | Subclass feature | 6 | 5 | 10 | 4 | 3 | 3 | — | — | — | — | — | — |
| 7 | +3 | Sorcery Incarnate | 7 | 5 | 11 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 1 | — | — | — | — | — |
| 8 | +3 | Ability Score Improvement | 8 | 5 | 12 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 2 | — | — | — | — | — |
| 9 | +4 | — | 9 | 5 | 14 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 1 | — | — | — | — |
| 10 | +4 | Metamagic | 10 | 6 | 15 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | — | — | — | — |
| 11 | +4 | — | 11 | 6 | 16 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | — | — | — |
| 12 | +4 | Ability Score Improvement | 12 | 6 | 16 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | — | — | — |
| 13 | +5 | — | 13 | 6 | 17 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 | — | — |
| 14 | +5 | Subclass feature | 14 | 6 | 17 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 | — | — |
| 15 | +5 | — | 15 | 6 | 18 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 | 1 | — |
| 16 | +5 | Ability Score Improvement | 16 | 6 | 18 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 | 1 | — |
| 17 | +6 | Metamagic | 17 | 6 | 19 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| 18 | +6 | Subclass feature | 18 | 6 | 20 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| 19 | +6 | Epic Boon | 19 | 6 | 21 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| 20 | +6 | Arcane Apotheosis | 20 | 6 | 22 | 4 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 1 | 1 |

#### Level 1: Spellcasting

Drawing from your innate magic, you can cast spells. See "Spells" for the rules on spellcasting. The information below details how you use those rules with Sorcerer spells, which appear in the Sorcerer spell list later in the class's description.

_Cantrips._ You know four Sorcerer cantrips of your choice. [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Light|Light]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Prestidigitation|Prestidigitation]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Shocking Grasp|Shocking Grasp]], and [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Sorcerous Burst|Sorcerous Burst]] are recommended. Whenever you gain a Sorcerer level, you can replace one of your cantrips from this feature with another Sorcerer cantrip of your choice.

When you reach Sorcerer levels 4 and 10, you learn another Sorcerer cantrip of your choice, as shown in the Cantrips column of the Sorcerer Features table.

_Spell Slots._ The Sorcerer Features table shows how many spell slots you have to cast your level 1+ spells. You regain all expended slots when you finish a Long Rest.

_Prepared Spells of Level 1+._ You prepare the list of level 1+ spells that are available for you to cast with this feature. To start, choose two level 1 Sorcerer spells. [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Burning Hands|Burning Hands]] and [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Detect Magic|Detect Magic]] are recommended.

The number of spells on your list increases as you gain Sorcerer levels, as shown in the Prepared Spells column of the Sorcerer Features table. Whenever that number increases, choose additional Sorcerer spells until the number of spells on your list matches the number in the Sorcerer Features table. The chosen spells must be of a level for which you have spell slots. For example, if you're a level 3 Sorcerer, your list of prepared spells can include six Sorcerer spells of level 1 or 2 in any combination.

If another Sorcerer feature gives you spells that you always have prepared, those spells don't count against the number of spells you can prepare with this feature, but those spells otherwise count as Sorcerer spells for you.

_Changing Your Prepared Spells._ Whenever you gain a Sorcerer level, you can replace one spell on your list with another Sorcerer spell for which you have spell slots.

_Spellcasting Ability._ Charisma is your spellcasting ability for your Sorcerer spells.

_Spellcasting Focus._ You can use an Arcane Focus as a Spellcasting Focus for your Sorcerer spells.

#### Level 1: Innate Sorcery

An event in your past left an indelible mark on you, infusing you with simmering magic. As a Bonus Action, you can unleash that magic for 1 minute, during which you gain the following benefits:
• The spell save DC of your Sorcerer spells increases by 1.
• You have Advantage on the attack rolls of Sorcerer spells you cast.

You can use this feature twice, and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 2: Font of Magic

You can tap into the wellspring of magic within yourself. This wellspring is represented by Sorcery Points, which allow you to create a variety of magical effects.

You have 2 Sorcery Points, and you gain more as you reach higher levels, as shown in the Sorcery Points column of the Sorcerer Features table. You can't have more Sorcery Points than the number shown in the table for your level. You regain all expended Sorcery Points when you finish a Long Rest.

You can use your Sorcery Points to fuel the options below, along with other features, such as Metamagic, that use those points.

_Converting Spell Slots to Sorcery Points._ You can expend a spell slot to gain a number of Sorcery Points equal to the slot's level (no action required).

_Creating Spell Slots._ As a Bonus Action, you can transform unexpended Sorcery Points into one spell slot. The Creating Spell Slots table shows the cost of creating a spell slot of a given level, and it lists the minimum Sorcerer level you must be to create a slot. You can create a spell slot no higher than level 5.

Any spell slot you create with this feature vanishes when you finish a Long Rest.

**Creating Spell Slots**

| Spell Slot Level | Sorcery Point Cost | Min. Sorcerer Level |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 2 | 2 |
| 2 | 3 | 3 |
| 3 | 5 | 5 |
| 4 | 6 | 7 |
| 5 | 7 | 9 |

#### Level 2: Metamagic

Because your magic flows from within, you can alter your spells to suit your needs; you gain two Metamagic options of your choice from "Metamagic Options" later in this class's description. You use the chosen options to temporarily modify spells you cast. To use an option, you must spend the number of Sorcery Points that it costs.

You can use only one Metamagic option on a spell when you cast it unless otherwise noted in one of those options.

Whenever you gain a Sorcerer level, you can replace one of your Metamagic options with one you don't know. You gain two more options at Sorcerer level 10 and two more at Sorcerer level 17.

#### Level 3: Sorcerer Subclass

You gain a Sorcerer subclass of your choice. The Draconic Sorcery subclass is detailed after this class's description. A subclass is a specialization that grants you features at certain Sorcerer levels. For the rest of your career, you gain each of your subclass's features that are of your Sorcerer level or lower.

#### Level 4: Ability Score Improvement

You gain the Ability Score Improvement feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. You gain this feature again at Sorcerer levels 8, 12, and 16.

#### Level 5: Sorcerous Restoration

When you finish a Short Rest, you can regain expended Sorcery Points, but no more than a number equal to half your Sorcerer level (round down). Once you use this feature, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 7: Sorcery Incarnate

If you have no uses of Innate Sorcery left, you can use it if you spend 2 Sorcery Points when you take the Bonus Action to activate it.

In addition, while your Innate Sorcery feature is active, you can use up to two of your Metamagic options on each spell you cast.

#### Level 19: Epic Boon

You gain an Epic Boon feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. Boon of Dimensional Travel is recommended.

#### Level 20: Arcane Apotheosis

While your Innate Sorcery feature is active, you can use one Metamagic option on each of your turns without spending Sorcery Points on it.

### Metamagic Options

The following options are available to your Metamagic feature. The options are presented in alphabetical order.

#### Careful Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you cast a spell that forces other creatures to make a saving throw, you can protect some of those creatures from the spell's full force. To do so, spend 1 Sorcery Point and choose a number of those creatures up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of one creature). A chosen creature automatically succeeds on its saving throw against the spell, and
it takes no damage if it would normally take half damage on a successful save.

#### Distant Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you cast a spell that has a range of at least 5 feet, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to double the spell's range. Or when you cast a spell that has a range of Touch, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to make the spell's range 30 feet.

#### Empowered Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you roll damage for a spell, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to reroll a number of the damage dice up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of one), and you must use the new rolls.

You can use Empowered Spell even if you've already used a different Metamagic option during the casting of the spell.

#### Extended Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you cast a spell that has a duration of 1 minute or longer, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to double its duration to a maximum duration of 24 hours.

If the affected spell requires Concentration, you have Advantage on any saving throw you make to maintain that Concentration.

#### Heightened Spell

_Cost: 2 Sorcery Points_

When you cast a spell that forces a creature to make a saving throw, you can spend 2 Sorcery Points to give one target of the spell Disadvantage on saves against the spell.

#### Quickened Spell

_Cost: 2 Sorcery Points_

When you cast a spell that has a casting time of an action, you can spend 2 Sorcery Points to change the casting time to a Bonus Action for this casting. You can't modify a spell in this way if you've already cast a level 1+ spell on the current turn, nor can you cast a level 1+ spell on this turn after modifying a spell in this way.

#### Seeking Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

If you make an attack roll for a spell and miss, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to reroll the d20, and you must use the new roll.

You can use Seeking Spell even if you've already used a different Metamagic option during the casting of the spell.

#### Subtle Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you cast a spell, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to cast it without any Verbal, Somatic, or Material components, except Material components that are consumed by the spell or that have a cost specified in the spell.

#### Transmuted Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you cast a spell that deals a type of damage from the following list, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to change that damage type to one of the other listed types: Acid, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Poison, Thunder.

#### Twinned Spell

_Cost: 1 Sorcery Point_

When you cast a spell, such as [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Charm Person|Charm Person]], that can be cast with a higher-level spell slot to target an additional creature, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to increase the spell's effective level by 1.
