### Sorcerer Subclass: Aberrant Sorcery

_Channel a Psionic Taint from the Far Realm_

Your innate magic carries a psionic taint from the Far Realm, a legacy passed down through your bloodline. This otherworldly gift manifests as telepathy, psychic resilience, and the eventual ability to warp your own body.

#### Level 3: Psionic Spells

When you reach a Sorcerer level specified in the Psionic Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Psionic Spells**

| Sorcerer Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Arms of Hadar\|Arms of Hadar]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Calm Emotions\|Calm Emotions]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Detect Thoughts\|Detect Thoughts]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Dissonant Whispers\|Dissonant Whispers]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mind Sliver\|Mind Sliver]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hunger of Hadar\|Hunger of Hadar]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Sending\|Sending]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Black Tentacles\|Evard's Black Tentacles]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Aberration\|Summon Aberration]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Telepathic Bond\|Rary's Telepathic Bond]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Telekinesis\|Telekinesis]] |

#### Level 3: Telepathic Speech

As a Bonus Action, you choose one creature you can see within 30 feet of yourself. You and that creature can communicate telepathically with each other for a number of minutes equal to your Sorcerer level, as long as you're within a number of miles of each other equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1 mile). To understand each other, you and the creature must share a language. This effect ends early if you use this feature again.

#### Level 6: Psionic Sorcery

When you cast any level 1 or higher spell from your Psionic Spells feature, you can cast it by spending a number of Sorcery Points equal to the spell's level, rather than expending a spell slot. A spell cast in this way doesn't require Verbal or Somatic components, though any costly or consumed Material component is still required.

#### Level 6: Psychic Defenses

You have Resistance to Psychic damage, and you have Advantage on saving throws you make to avoid or end the Charmed or Frightened condition.

#### Level 14: Revelation in Flesh

As a Bonus Action, you can spend 1 or more Sorcery Points to magically alter your body for 10 minutes. For each point you spend, choose one of the following benefits, which lasts until the transformation ends.

**Aquatic Adaptation.** You have a Swim Speed equal to twice your Speed, and you can breathe underwater.

**Glistening Flight.** You have a Fly Speed equal to your Speed, and you can hover.

**See the Invisible.** You can see invisible creatures and objects within 60 feet of yourself as though they weren't invisible.

**Wormlike Movement.** You can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without squeezing, and you have Advantage on ability checks and saving throws you make to escape the Grappled condition or the Restrained condition.

#### Level 18: Warping Implosion

As a Magic action, you teleport to an unoccupied space you can see within 120 feet of yourself. Immediately after you disappear, each creature within 30 feet of the space you left must make a Strength saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, a creature takes 3d10 Force damage and is pulled to the nearest unoccupied space next to the space you left. On a successful save, a creature takes half as much damage only.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend 5 Sorcery Points (no action required) to use it again.

### Sorcerer Subclass: Clockwork Sorcery

_Draw Order from Mechanus_

Your innate magic is attuned to Mechanus, the plane of absolute order, and it flows through you as a force that shores up allies against chance, cancels out ill fortune, and eventually mends the world around you.

#### Level 3: Clockwork Spells

When you reach a Sorcerer level specified in the Clockwork Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Clockwork Spells**

| Sorcerer Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Aid\|Aid]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Alarm\|Alarm]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Lesser Restoration\|Lesser Restoration]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Protection from Evil and Good\|Protection from Evil and Good]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Dispel Magic\|Dispel Magic]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Protection from Energy\|Protection from Energy]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Freedom of Movement\|Freedom of Movement]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Construct\|Summon Construct]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Greater Restoration\|Greater Restoration]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Wall of Force\|Wall of Force]] |

#### Level 3: Restore Balance

When a creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself is about to roll a d20 with Advantage or Disadvantage, you can take a Reaction to prevent that roll from being affected by Advantage and Disadvantage. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 6: Bastion of Law

As a Magic action, you expend 1 to 5 Sorcery Points to create a magical ward on yourself or another creature you can see within 30 feet of yourself. The ward is represented by a number of d8s equal to the number of Sorcery Points spent. Whenever the warded creature takes damage, it can expend one or more of those dice (no action required), roll them, and reduce the damage taken by the total rolled. The ward lasts until you finish a Long Rest or until you use this feature again.

#### Level 14: Trance of Order

As a Bonus Action, you enter a trance for 1 minute. While in the trance, attack rolls against you can't benefit from Advantage, and whenever you make a D20 Test, you can treat a roll of 9 or lower on the d20 as a 10.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend 5 Sorcery Points (no action required) to use it again.

#### Level 18: Clockwork Cavalcade

As a Magic action, you summon spirits of order in a 30-foot Cube originating from yourself, appearing only in unoccupied spaces. The spirits create the following effects within the Cube.

_Restoration._ Restore up to 100 Hit Points, divided as you choose among any number of creatures there.

_Repair._ Repair all damaged objects there.

_Dispel Magic._ Dispel every spell of level 6 or lower affecting a creature or an object there (your choice of which spells if more than one qualifies).

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend 7 Sorcery Points (no action required) to use it again.

### Sorcerer Subclass: Draconic Sorcery

_Breathe the Magic of Dragons_

Your innate magic comes from the gift of a dragon. Perhaps an ancient dragon facing death bequeathed some of its magical power to you or your ancestor. You might have absorbed magic from a site infused with dragons' power. Or perhaps you handled a treasure taken from a dragon's hoard that was steeped in draconic power. Or you might have a dragon for an ancestor.

#### Level 3: Draconic Resilience

The magic in your body manifests physical traits of your draconic gift. Your Hit Point maximum increases by 3, and it increases by 1 whenever you gain another Sorcerer level.

Parts of you are also covered by dragon-like scales. While you aren't wearing armor, your base Armor Class equals 10 plus your Dexterity and Charisma modifiers.

#### Level 3: Draconic Spells

When you reach a Sorcerer level specified in the Draconic Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Draconic Spells**

| Sorcerer Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Alter Self\|Alter Self]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Chromatic Orb\|Chromatic Orb]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Command\|Command]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Dragon's Breath\|Dragon's Breath]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Fear\|Fear]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Fly\|Fly]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Arcane Eye\|Arcane Eye]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Charm Monster\|Charm Monster]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Legend Lore\|Legend Lore]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Dragon\|Summon Dragon]] |

#### Level 6: Elemental Affinity

Your draconic magic has an affinity with a damage type associated with dragons. Choose one of those types: Acid, Cold, Fire, Lightning, or Poison.

You have Resistance to that damage type, and when you cast a spell that deals damage of that type, you can add your Charisma modifier to one damage roll of that spell.

#### Level 14: Dragon Wings

As a Bonus Action, you can cause draconic wings to appear on your back. The wings last for 1 hour or until you dismiss them (no action required). For the duration, you have a Fly Speed of 60 feet.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest unless you spend 3 Sorcery Points (no action required) to restore your use of it.

#### Level 18: Dragon Companion

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Dragon|Summon Dragon]] without a Material component. You can also cast it once without a spell slot, and you regain the ability to cast it in this way when you finish a Long Rest.

Whenever you start casting the spell, you can modify it so that it doesn't require Concentration. If you do so, the spell's duration becomes 1 minute for that casting.

### Sorcerer Subclass: Wild Magic Sorcery

_Wield Unpredictable, Chaotic Power_

Your innate magic is unpredictable and volatile, liable to erupt in chaotic surges. You can learn to lean into that chaos and, in time, begin to tame it.

#### Level 3: Tides of Chaos

You can manipulate the forces of chance and chaos to gain Advantage on one D20 Test of your choice before you roll the d20. Once you do so, you must cast a Sorcerer spell using a spell slot or finish a Long Rest before you can use this feature again. If you cast a Sorcerer spell using a spell slot before finishing a Long Rest, you immediately roll on the Wild Magic Surge table.

#### Level 3: Wild Magic Surge

Once on each of your turns, immediately after you cast a Sorcerer spell using a spell slot, you can roll 1d20. If you roll a 20, you immediately roll on the Wild Magic Surge table to create a magical effect. If the effect is a spell, it's too wild to be affected by your Metamagic.

**Wild Magic Surge**

| d100 | Effect |
| --- | --- |
| 01–04 | Roll on this table at the start of each of your turns for the next minute, ignoring this result on subsequent rolls. |
| 05–08 | A friendly creature appears in a random unoccupied space within 60 feet of you, vanishing after 1 minute. Roll 1d4: 1 Modron Duodrone, 2 Flumph, 3 Modron Monodrone, 4 Unicorn. |
| 09–12 | For the next minute, you regain 5 Hit Points at the start of each of your turns. |
| 13–16 | Creatures have Disadvantage on saving throws against the next spell you cast in the next minute that involves a saving throw. |
| 17–20 | An effect lasting 1 minute (roll 1d8): 1 faint ethereal music follows you, 2 you grow one size category, 3 a feathered beard grows on you until you sneeze, 4 you must shout when speaking, 5 illusory butterflies flutter within 10 feet of you, 6 an eye appears on your forehead, giving you Advantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks, 7 pink bubbles float from your mouth when you speak, 8 your skin turns vibrant blue for 24 hours or until removed by Remove Curse. |
| 21–24 | For the next minute, all your spells that have a casting time of an action instead have a casting time of a Bonus Action. |
| 25–28 | You are transported to the Astral Plane until the end of your next turn, then you return to the space you left or the nearest unoccupied space. |
| 29–32 | The next damage-dealing spell you cast within the next minute doesn't roll damage dice; use the highest number possible for each die instead. |
| 33–36 | You have Resistance to all damage for the next minute. |
| 37–40 | You turn into a potted plant until the start of your next turn. While a plant, you have the Incapacitated condition and Vulnerability to all damage. |
| 41–44 | For the next minute, you can teleport up to 20 feet as a Bonus Action on each of your turns. |
| 45–48 | You and up to three creatures you choose within 30 feet of you have the Invisible condition for 1 minute. The condition ends on a creature after it makes an attack roll, deals damage, or casts a spell. |
| 49–52 | A spectral shield hovers near you for the next minute, granting you a +2 bonus to Armor Class and Immunity to the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Magic Missile\|Magic Missile]] spell. |
| 53–56 | You can take one extra action on this turn. |
| 57–60 | You cast a random spell without expending a spell slot and without needing Concentration, using its full duration (roll 1d10): 1 Confusion, 2 Fireball, 3 Fog Cloud, 4 Fly (on a random creature within 60 feet), 5 Grease, 6 Levitate (on yourself), 7 Magic Missile (as if cast with a 5th-level slot), 8 Mirror Image, 9 Polymorph (on yourself; you become a Goat if you fail the save), 10 See Invisibility. |
| 61–64 | For the next minute, any flammable, nonmagical object you touch that isn't worn or carried by another creature bursts into flame, taking 1d4 Fire damage and igniting. |
| 65–68 | If you die within the next hour, you immediately come back to life as though by the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Reincarnate\|Reincarnate]] spell. |
| 69–72 | You have the Frightened condition until the end of your next turn. |
| 73–76 | You teleport up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space you can see. |
| 77–80 | A random creature within 60 feet of you has the Poisoned condition for 1d4 hours. |
| 81–84 | You radiate Bright Light in a 30-foot radius for the next minute. Any creature that ends its turn within 5 feet of you has the Blinded condition until the end of its next turn. |
| 85–88 | Up to three creatures you choose within 30 feet of you take 1d10 Necrotic damage, and you regain Hit Points equal to the total Necrotic damage dealt. |
| 89–92 | Up to three creatures you choose within 30 feet of you take 4d10 Lightning damage. |
| 93–96 | You and all creatures within 30 feet of you have Vulnerability to Piercing damage for the next minute. |
| 97–00 | Roll 1d6: 1 you regain 2d10 Hit Points, 2 one ally within 300 feet regains 2d10 Hit Points, 3 you regain your lowest-level expended spell slot, 4 one ally regains their lowest-level expended spell slot, 5 you regain all expended Sorcery Points, 6 all effects of the 17–20 entry above happen to you simultaneously. |

#### Level 6: Bend Luck

Immediately after another creature you can see rolls a d20 for a D20 Test, you can take a Reaction and spend 1 Sorcery Point to roll 1d4 and apply the number rolled as a bonus or penalty (your choice) to that roll.

#### Level 14: Controlled Chaos

Whenever you roll on the Wild Magic Surge table, you can roll twice and use either number rolled.

#### Level 18: Tamed Surge

Immediately after you cast a Sorcerer spell using a spell slot, you can create an effect of your choice from the Wild Magic Surge table instead of rolling on that table. You can't choose the final entry (97–00) this way, and if the chosen effect involves a roll, you must make that roll. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Sorcerer Subclass: Divine Soul

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Sorcerer subclasses (Aberrant/Clockwork/Draconic/Wild Magic)—kept here as bonus content. (2024 phrasing note: the original 2014-era subclass granted its first two features at Sorcerer level 1; this vault's 2024-style Sorcerer doesn't choose a subclass until level 3, so both are presented below as Level 3, matching how the core 2024 subclasses are structured. The other legacy Sorcerer, Warlock, and Wizard subclasses below follow the same convention without repeating this note.)_

_Channel Divine Power through Sorcery_

Your innate magic springs from a divine source rather than a mortal bloodline—an angel, a god's blessing, or some other brush with the divine planes.

#### Level 3: Divine Magic

Whenever your Spellcasting feature lets you learn a Sorcerer cantrip or spell, you can instead choose it from the Cleric spell list. In addition, choose an affinity—Good, Evil, Law, Chaos, or Neutrality—and learn one bonus spell tied to it that doesn't count against your spells known: [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Cure Wounds|Cure Wounds]] (Good), [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Inflict Wounds|Inflict Wounds]] (Evil), [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Bless|Bless]] (Law), [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Bane|Bane]] (Chaos), or [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Protection from Evil and Good|Protection from Evil and Good]] (Neutrality).

#### Level 3: Favored by the Gods

If you fail a saving throw or miss with an attack roll, you can roll 2d4 and add it to the total, potentially changing the outcome. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

#### Level 6: Empowered Healing

Once per turn, if you aren't Incapacitated, you can spend 1 Sorcery Point to reroll any number of dice used to determine Hit Points restored by a spell that you or an ally within 5 feet of yourself casts.

#### Level 14: Otherworldly Wings

As a Bonus Action, you can manifest spectral wings, granting yourself a Fly Speed of 30 feet. The wings last until you have the Incapacitated condition, until you die, or until you dismiss them (a Bonus Action). Their appearance reflects your affinity: feathered eagle wings (Good or Law), bat wings (Evil or Chaos), or dragonfly wings (Neutrality).

#### Level 18: Unearthly Recovery

As a Bonus Action when you have fewer than half your Hit Points remaining, you can regain Hit Points equal to half your Hit Point maximum. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Sorcerer Subclass: Shadow Magic

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Sorcerer subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Draw Power from the Shadowfell_

Your innate magic is touched by the Shadowfell, granting you keen sight in darkness and an uncanny resilience against death itself.

#### Level 3: Eyes of the Dark

You have Darkvision with a range of 120 feet. Starting at Sorcerer level 3, you know the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Darkness|Darkness]] spell, which doesn't count against your spells known; you can cast it by spending 2 Sorcery Points, instead of expending a spell slot, and when you cast it this way, you can see through the Darkness it creates.

#### Level 3: Strength of the Grave

When damage would reduce you to 0 Hit Points and doesn't kill you outright, you can make a Charisma saving throw (DC 5 plus the damage taken). On a success, you drop to 1 Hit Point instead. This feature doesn't work against Radiant damage or a Critical Hit. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 6: Hound of Ill Omen

As a Bonus Action, you can spend 3 Sorcery Points to summon a spectral hound (using the dire wolf stat block) that appears within 60 feet of a creature you can see and immediately uses its whole movement to move toward that creature. The hound has Temporary Hit Points equal to half your Sorcerer level, can move through creatures and objects as though they were Difficult Terrain, and while within 5 feet of its target, that target has Disadvantage on saving throws against your spells. The hound lasts for 1 minute or until it's reduced to 0 Hit Points.

#### Level 14: Shadow Walk

While you're in Dim Light or Darkness, you can take a Bonus Action to teleport up to 120 feet to an unoccupied space you can see that is also in Dim Light or Darkness.

#### Level 18: Umbral Form

As a Bonus Action, you can spend 6 Sorcery Points to transform into a shadowy form for 1 minute. While transformed, you have Resistance to all damage except Force and Radiant damage, and you can move through other creatures and objects as though they were Difficult Terrain.

### Sorcerer Subclass: Storm Sorcery

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Sorcerer subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Command the Fury of Wind and Lightning_

Your innate magic is tied to the elemental forces of storms, and you carry the ability to speak with primordial spirits and ride upon the wind itself.

#### Level 3: Wind Speaker

You can speak, read, and write Primordial, along with its dialects (Aquan, Auran, Ignan, and Terran).

#### Level 3: Tempestuous Magic

As a Bonus Action immediately before or after you cast a spell of level 1 or higher, you can fly up to 10 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

#### Level 6: Heart of the Storm

You have Resistance to Lightning damage and Thunder damage. In addition, whenever you start casting a spell of level 1 or higher that deals Lightning or Thunder damage, creatures of your choice within 10 feet of yourself take damage (Lightning or Thunder, your choice) equal to half your Sorcerer level.

#### Level 6: Storm Guide

If it's raining, you can take an action to cause the rain to stop falling in a 20-foot-radius Sphere centered on yourself; you can resume the rain as a Bonus Action. If wind is blowing, you can choose the wind's direction in a 100-foot-radius Sphere centered on yourself, using a Bonus Action each round to maintain the choice.

#### Level 14: Storm's Fury

When you're hit by a melee attack, you can take a Reaction to deal Lightning damage equal to your Sorcerer level to the attacker; the attacker must also make a Strength saving throw against your spell save DC or be pushed 20 feet away from you.

#### Level 18: Wind Soul

You have Immunity to Lightning and Thunder damage, and you have a magical Fly Speed of 60 feet. Once per Short or Long Rest, as an action, you can grant a Fly Speed of 30 feet for 1 hour to a number of creatures within 30 feet of yourself (which can include you) equal to 3 plus your Charisma modifier; while this benefit is in effect for others, your own Fly Speed drops to 30 feet for the same duration.

## Warlock

**Core Warlock Traits**

| Col 1 | Col 2 |
| --- | --- |
| Primary Ability | Charisma |
| Hit Point Die | D8 per Warlock level |
| Saving Throw Proficiencies | Wisdom and Charisma |
| Skill Proficiencies | Choose 2: Arcana, Deception, History, Intimidation, Investigation, Nature, or Religion |
| Weapon Proficiencies | Simple weapons |
| Armor Training | Light armor |
| Starting Equipment | Choose A or B: (A) Leather Armor, Sickle, 2 Daggers, Arcane Focus (orb), Book (occult lore), Scholar's Pack, and 15 GP; or (B) 100 GP |

### Becoming a Warlock …

#### As a Level 1 Character

- Gain all the traits in the Core Warlock Traits table.
- Gain the Warlock's level 1 features, which are listed in the Warlock Features table.

#### As a Multiclass Character

- Gain the following traits from the Core Warlock Traits table: Hit Point Die and training with Light armor.
- Gain the Warlock's level 1 features, which are listed in the Warlock Features table. See the multiclassing rules in "Character Creation" to determine your available spell slots.

### Warlock Class Features

As a Warlock, you gain the following class features when you reach the specified Warlock levels. These features are listed in the Warlock Features table.

**Warlock Features**

| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Class Features | Eldritch Invocations | Cantrips | Prepared Spells | Spell Slots | Slot Level |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | +2 | Eldritch Invocations, Pact Magic | 1 | 2 | 2 | 1 | 1 |
| 2 | +2 | Magical Cunning | 3 | 2 | 3 | 2 | 1 |
| 3 | +2 | Warlock Subclass | 3 | 2 | 4 | 2 | 2 |
| 4 | +2 | Ability Score Improvement | 3 | 3 | 5 | 2 | 2 |
| 5 | +3 | — | 5 | 3 | 6 | 2 | 3 |
| 6 | +3 | Subclass feature | 5 | 3 | 7 | 2 | 3 |
| 7 | +3 | — | 6 | 3 | 8 | 2 | 4 |
| 8 | +3 | Ability Score Improvement | 6 | 3 | 9 | 2 | 4 |
| 9 | +4 | Contact Patron | 7 | 3 | 10 | 2 | 5 |
| 10 | +4 | Subclass feature | 7 | 4 | 10 | 2 | 5 |
| 11 | +4 | Mystic Arcanum (level 6 spell) | 7 | 4 | 11 | 3 | 5 |
| 12 | +4 | Ability Score Improvement | 8 | 4 | 11 | 3 | 5 |
| 13 | +5 | Mystic Arcanum (level 7 spell) | 8 | 4 | 12 | 3 | 5 |
| 14 | +5 | Subclass feature | 8 | 4 | 12 | 3 | 5 |
| 15 | +5 | Mystic Arcanum (level 8 spell) | 9 | 4 | 13 | 3 | 5 |
| 16 | +5 | Ability Score Improvement | 9 | 4 | 13 | 3 | 5 |
| 17 | +6 | Mystic Arcanum (level 9 spell) | 9 | 4 | 14 | 4 | 5 |
| 18 | +6 | — | 10 | 4 | 14 | 4 | 5 |
| 19 | +6 | Epic Boon | 10 | 4 | 15 | 4 | 5 |
| 20 | +6 | Eldritch Master | 10 | 4 | 15 | 4 | 5 |

#### Level 1: Eldritch Invocations

You have unearthed Eldritch Invocations, pieces of forbidden knowledge that imbue you with an abiding magical ability or other lessons. You gain one invocation of your choice, such as Pact of the Tome. Invocations are described in the "Eldritch Invocation Options" section later in this class's description.

_Prerequisites._ If an invocation has a prerequisite, you must meet it to learn that invocation. For example, if an invocation requires you to be a level 5+ Warlock, you can select the invocation once you reach Warlock level 5.

_Replacing and Gaining Invocations._ Whenever you gain a Warlock level, you can replace one of your invocations with another one for which you qualify. You can't replace an invocation if it's a prerequisite for another invocation that you have.

When you gain certain Warlock levels, you gain more invocations of your choice, as shown in the Invocations column of the Warlock Features table.

You can't pick the same invocation more than once unless its description says otherwise.

#### Level 1: Pact Magic

Through occult ceremony, you have formed a pact with a mysterious entity to gain magical powers. The entity is a voice in the shadows—its identity unclear—but its boon to you is concrete: the ability to cast spells. See "Spells" for the rules on spellcasting. The information below details how you use those rules with Warlock spells, which appear in the Warlock spell list later in the class's description.

_Cantrips._ You know two Warlock cantrips of your choice. [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Eldritch Blast|Eldritch Blast]] and [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Prestidigitation|Prestidigitation]] are recommended. Whenever you gain a Warlock level, you can replace one of your cantrips from this feature with another Warlock cantrip of your choice.

When you reach Warlock levels 4 and 10, you learn another Warlock cantrip of your choice, as shown in the Cantrips column of the Warlock Features table.

_Spell Slots._ The Warlock Features table shows how many spell slots you have to cast your Warlock spells of levels 1–5. The table also shows the level of those slots, all of which are the same level. You regain all expended Pact Magic spell slots when you finish a Short or Long Rest.

For example, when you're a level 5 Warlock, you have two level 3 spell slots. To cast the level 1 spell [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Charm Person|Charm Person]], you must spend one of those slots, and you cast it as a level 3 spell.

_Prepared Spells of Level 1+._ You prepare the list of level 1+ spells that are available for you to cast with this feature. To start, choose two level 1 Warlock spells. [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Charm Person|Charm Person]] and [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hex|Hex]] are recommended.

The number of spells on your list increases as you gain Warlock levels, as shown in the Prepared Spells column of the Warlock Features table. Whenever that number increases, choose additional Warlock spells until the number of spells on your list matches the number in the table. The chosen spells must be of a level no higher than what's shown in the table's Slot Level column for your level. When you reach level 6, for example, you learn a new Warlock spell, which can be of levels 1–3.

If another Warlock feature gives you spells that you always have prepared, those spells don't count against the number of spells you can prepare with this feature, but those spells otherwise count as Warlock spells for you.

_Changing Your Prepared Spells._ Whenever you gain a Warlock level, you can replace one spell on your list with another Warlock spell of an eligible level.

_Spellcasting Ability._ Charisma is the spellcasting ability for your Warlock spells.

_Spellcasting Focus._ You can use an Arcane Focus as a Spellcasting Focus for your Warlock spells.

#### Level 2: Magical Cunning

You can perform an esoteric rite for 1 minute. At the end of it, you regain expended Pact Magic spell slots but no more than a number equal to half your maximum (round up). Once you use this feature, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 3: Warlock Subclass

You gain a Warlock subclass of your choice. The Fiend Patron subclass is detailed after this class's description. A subclass is a specialization that grants you features at certain Warlock levels. For the rest of your career, you gain each of your subclass's features that are of your Warlock level or lower.

#### Level 4: Ability Score Improvement

You gain the Ability Score Improvement feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. You gain this feature again at Warlock levels 8, 12, and 16.

#### Level 9: Contact Patron

In the past, you usually contacted your patron through intermediaries. Now you can communicate directly; you always have the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Contact Other Plane|Contact Other Plane]] spell prepared. With this feature, you can cast the spell without expending a spell slot to contact your patron, and you automatically succeed on the spell's saving throw.

Once you cast the spell with this feature, you can't do so in this way again until you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 11: Mystic Arcanum

Your patron grants you a magical secret called an arcanum. Choose one level 6 Warlock spell as this arcanum.

You can cast your arcanum spell once without expending a spell slot, and you must finish a Long Rest before you can cast it in this way again.

As shown in the Warlock Features table, you gain another Warlock spell of your choice that can be cast in this way when you reach Warlock levels 13 (level 7 spell), 15 (level 8 spell), and 17 (level 9 spell). You regain all uses of your Mystic Arcanum when you finish a Long Rest.

Whenever you gain a Warlock level, you can replace one of your arcanum spells with another Warlock spell of the same level.

#### Level 19: Epic Boon

You gain an Epic Boon feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. Boon of Fate is recommended.

#### Level 20: Eldritch Master

When you use your Magical Cunning feature, you regain all your expended Pact Magic spell slots.

### Eldritch Invocation Options

Eldritch Invocation options appear in alphabetical order.

#### Agonizing Blast

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock, a Warlock Cantrip That Deals Damage_

Choose one of your known Warlock cantrips that deals damage. You can add your Charisma modifier to that spell's damage rolls.

_Repeatable._ You can gain this invocation more than once. Each time you do so, choose a different eligible cantrip.

#### Armor of Shadows

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Armor|Mage Armor]] on yourself without expending a spell slot.

#### Ascendant Step

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Levitate|Levitate]] on yourself without expending a spell slot.

#### Devil's Sight

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock_

You can see normally in Dim Light and Darkness—both magical and nonmagical—within 120 feet of yourself.

#### Devouring Blade

_Prerequisite: Level 12+ Warlock, Thirsting Blade Invocation_

The Extra Attack of your Thirsting Blade invocation confers two extra attacks rather than one.

#### Eldritch Mind

You have Advantage on Constitution saving throws that you make to maintain Concentration.

#### Eldritch Smite

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock, Pact of the Blade Invocation_

Once per turn when you hit a creature with your pact weapon, you can expend a Pact Magic spell slot to deal an extra 1d8 Force damage to the target,
plus another 1d8 per level of the spell slot, and you can give the target the Prone condition if it is Huge or smaller.

#### Eldritch Spear

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock, a Warlock Cantrip That Deals Damage_

Choose one of your known Warlock cantrips that deals damage and has a range of 10+ feet. When you cast that spell, its range increases by a number of feet equal to 30 times your Warlock level.

_Repeatable._ You can gain this invocation more than once. Each time you do so, choose a different eligible cantrip.

#### Fiendish Vigor

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#False Life|False Life]] on yourself without expending a spell slot. When you cast the spell with this feature, you don't roll the die for the Temporary Hit Points; you automatically get the highest number on the die.

#### Gaze of Two Minds

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock_

You can use a Bonus Action to touch a willing creature and perceive through its senses until the end of your next turn. As long as the creature is on the same plane of existence as you, you can take a Bonus Action on subsequent turns to maintain this connection, extending the duration until the end of your next turn. The connection ends if you don't maintain it in this way.

While perceiving through the other creature's senses, you benefit from any special senses possessed by that creature, and you can cast spells as if you were in your space or the other creature's space if the two of you are within 60 feet of each other.

#### Gift of the Depths

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock_

You can breathe underwater, and you gain a Swim Speed equal to your Speed.

You can also cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Water Breathing|Water Breathing]] once without expending a spell slot. You regain the ability to cast it in this way again when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Gift of the Protectors

_Prerequisite: Level 9+ Warlock, Pact of the Tome Invocation_

A new page appears in your _Book of Shadows_ when you conjure it. With your permission, a creature can take an action to write its name on that page, which can contain a number of names equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of one name).

When any creature whose name is on the page is reduced to 0 Hit Points but not killed outright, the creature magically drops to 1 Hit Point instead. Once this magic is triggered, no creature can benefit from it until you finish a Long Rest.

As a Magic action, you can erase a name on the page by touching it.

#### Investment of the Chain Master

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock, Pact of the Chain Invocation_

When you cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Find Familiar|Find Familiar]], you infuse the summoned familiar with a measure of your eldritch power, granting the creature the following benefits.

_Aerial or Aquatic._ The familiar gains either a Fly Speed or a Swim Speed (your choice) of 40 feet.

_Quick Attack._ As a Bonus Action, you can command the familiar to take the Attack action.

_Necrotic or Radiant Damage._ Whenever the familiar deals Bludgeoning, Piercing, or Slashing damage, you can make it deal Necrotic or Radiant damage instead.

_Your Save DC._ If the familiar forces a creature to make a saving throw, it uses your spell save DC.

_Resistance._ When the familiar takes damage, you can take a Reaction to grant it Resistance against that damage.

#### Lessons of the First Ones

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock_

You have received knowledge from an elder entity of the multiverse, allowing you to gain one Origin feat of your choice (see "Feats").

_Repeatable._ You can gain this invocation more than once. Each time you do so, choose a different Origin feat.

#### Lifedrinker

_Prerequisite: Level 9+ Warlock, Pact of the Blade Invocation_

Once per turn when you hit a creature with your pact weapon, you can deal an extra 1d6 Necrotic, Psychic, or Radiant damage (your choice) to the creature, and you can expend one of your Hit Point Dice to roll it and regain a number of Hit Points equal to the roll plus your Constitution modifier (minimum of 1 Hit Point).

#### Mask of Many Faces

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Disguise Self|Disguise Self]] without expending a spell slot.

#### Master of Myriad Forms

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Alter Self|Alter Self]] without expending a spell slot.

#### Misty Visions

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Silent Image|Silent Image]] without expending a spell slot.

#### One with Shadows

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock_

While you're in an area of Dim Light or Darkness, you can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Invisibility|Invisibility]] on yourself without expending a spell slot.

#### Otherworldly Leap

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Jump|Jump]] on yourself without expending a spell slot.

#### Pact of the Blade

As a Bonus Action, you can conjure a pact weapon in your hand—a Simple or Martial Melee weapon of your choice with which you bond—or create a bond with a magic weapon you touch; you can't bond with a magic weapon if someone else is attuned to it or another Warlock is bonded with it. Until the bond ends, you have proficiency with the weapon, and you can use it as a Spellcasting Focus.

Whenever you attack with the bonded weapon, you can use your Charisma modifier for the attack and damage rolls instead of using Strength or Dexterity; and you can cause the weapon to deal Necrotic, Psychic, or Radiant damage or its normal damage type.

Your bond with the weapon ends if you use this feature's Bonus Action again, if the weapon is more than 5 feet away from you for 1 minute or more, or if you die. A conjured weapon disappears when the bond ends.

#### Pact of the Chain

You learn the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Find Familiar|Find Familiar]] spell and can cast it as a Magic action without expending a spell slot.

When you cast the spell, you choose one of the normal forms for your familiar or one of the following special forms: **Imp, Pseudodragon, Quasit, Skeleton, Sphinx of Wonder, Sprite,** or **Venomous Snake** (see "Monsters" for the familiar's stat block).

Additionally, when you take the Attack action, you can forgo one of your own attacks to allow your familiar to make one attack of its own with its Reaction.

#### Pact of the Tome

Stitching together strands of shadow, you conjure forth a book in your hand at the end of a Short or Long Rest. This _Book of Shadows_ (you determine its appearance) contains eldritch magic that only you can access, granting you the benefits below. The book disappears if you conjure another book with this feature or if you die.

_Cantrips and Rituals._ When the book appears, choose three cantrips, and choose two level 1 spells that have the Ritual tag. The spells can be from any class's spell list, and they must be spells you don't already have prepared. While the book is on your person, you have the chosen spells prepared, and they function as Warlock spells for you.

_Spellcasting Focus._ You can use the book as a Spellcasting Focus.

#### Repelling Blast

_Prerequisite: Level 2+ Warlock, a Warlock Cantrip That Deals Damage via an Attack Roll_

Choose one of your known Warlock cantrips that requires an attack roll. When you hit a Large or smaller creature with that cantrip, you can push the creature up to 10 feet straight away from you.

_Repeatable._ You can gain this invocation more than once. Each time you do so, choose a different eligible cantrip.

#### Thirsting Blade

_Prerequisite: Level 5+ Warlock, Pact of the Blade Invocation_

You gain the Extra Attack feature for your pact weapon only. With that feature, you can attack twice with the weapon instead of once when you take the Attack action on your turn.

#### Visions of Distant Realms

_Prerequisite: Level 9+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Arcane Eye|Arcane Eye]] without expending a spell slot.

#### Whispers of the Grave

_Prerequisite: Level 7+ Warlock_

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Speak with Dead|Speak with Dead]] without expending a spell slot.

#### Witch Sight

_Prerequisite: Level 15+ Warlock_

You have Truesight with a range of 30 feet.

This section presents the Warlock spell list. The spells are organized by spell level and then alphabetized, and each spell's school of magic is listed. In the Special column, _C_ means the spell requires Concentration, _R_ means it's a Ritual, and _M_ means it requires a specific Material component.

### Warlock Subclass: Archfey Patron

_Bargain with a Fey Noble_

Your patron is a lord or lady of the Feywild, a mysterious power whose motives are difficult for mortals to fathom. Their gifts let you slip between spaces and turn enchantment magic against your foes.

#### Level 3: Archfey Spells

When you reach a Warlock level specified in the Archfey Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Archfey Spells**

| Warlock Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Calm Emotions\|Calm Emotions]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Faerie Fire\|Faerie Fire]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Misty Step\|Misty Step]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Phantasmal Force\|Phantasmal Force]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Sleep\|Sleep]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Blink\|Blink]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Plant Growth\|Plant Growth]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Dominate Beast\|Dominate Beast]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Greater Invisibility\|Greater Invisibility]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Dominate Person\|Dominate Person]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Seeming\|Seeming]] |

#### Level 3: Steps of the Fey

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Misty Step|Misty Step]] without expending a spell slot a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

Whenever you cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Misty Step|Misty Step]] by any means, you can also cause one of the following effects.

**Refreshing Step.** You or one creature within 10 feet of yourself gains 1d10 Temporary Hit Points.

**Taunting Step.** Creatures within 5 feet of the space you left must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC or have Disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than you until the start of your next turn.

#### Level 6: Misty Escape

When you take damage, you can take a Reaction to cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Misty Step|Misty Step]] without expending a spell slot. When you cast the spell in this way, you can also cause one of the following effects.

**Disappearing Step.** You have the Invisible condition until the start of your next turn or until you make an attack roll, deal damage, or cast a spell.

**Dreadful Step.** Creatures within 5 feet of the space you left, or within 5 feet of the space you appear in, must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC or take 2d10 Psychic damage.

#### Level 10: Beguiling Defenses

You have Immunity to the Charmed condition. In addition, when a creature hits you with an attack roll, you can take a Reaction to reduce the damage you take from the attack by half (round down) and force the attacker to make a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, the attacker takes Psychic damage equal to the damage you took from the triggering attack.

Once you use this reaction, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend a Pact Magic spell slot (no action required) to use it again.

#### Level 14: Bewitching Magic

Immediately after you cast an Enchantment or Illusion spell using a spell slot and an action, you can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Misty Step|Misty Step]] as part of the same action, without expending a spell slot.

### Warlock Subclass: Celestial Patron

_Draw on Light from the Upper Planes_

Your patron is a being of the Upper Planes—an archangel, a solar, or another celestial power. Their light flows through you as healing and radiant fire, in place of the corruption other warlocks bargain for.

#### Level 3: Celestial Spells

When you reach a Warlock level specified in the Celestial Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Celestial Spells**

| Warlock Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Aid\|Aid]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Cure Wounds\|Cure Wounds]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Guiding Bolt\|Guiding Bolt]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Lesser Restoration\|Lesser Restoration]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Light\|Light]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Sacred Flame\|Sacred Flame]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Daylight\|Daylight]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Revivify\|Revivify]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Guardian of Faith\|Guardian of Faith]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Wall of Fire\|Wall of Fire]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Greater Restoration\|Greater Restoration]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Celestial\|Summon Celestial]] |

#### Level 3: Healing Light

You have a pool of d6s for healing that has a number of dice equal to 1 plus your Warlock level. As a Bonus Action, you can heal one creature within 60 feet of yourself, including yourself: expend a number of those d6s up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of one die), roll them, and the creature regains a number of Hit Points equal to the total rolled. This pool refills when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 6: Radiant Soul

You have Resistance to Radiant damage. In addition, once per turn when you cast a spell that deals Radiant or Fire damage, you can add your Charisma modifier to that spell's damage roll against one of its targets.

#### Level 10: Celestial Resilience

You gain Temporary Hit Points whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest, or whenever you use your Magical Cunning feature: you and up to five creatures you can see gain Temporary Hit Points. You gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Warlock level plus your Charisma modifier; each other chosen creature gains Temporary Hit Points equal to half your Warlock level (round down) plus your Charisma modifier.

#### Level 14: Searing Vengeance

When you or an ally within 60 feet of you is about to make a death saving throw, you can interrupt it to unleash a burst of radiant light. That creature instead regains Hit Points equal to half its Hit Point maximum, can end the Prone condition on itself, and stands up using no movement. Each creature of your choice within 30 feet of that creature takes 2d8 plus your Charisma modifier in Radiant damage and has the Blinded condition until the end of the current turn.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest.

### Warlock Subclass: Fiend Patron

_Make a Deal with the Lower Planes_

Your pact draws on the Lower Planes, the realms of perdition. You might forge a bargain with a demon lord, an archdevil, or another fiend that is especially mighty. That patron's aims are evil—the corruption or destruction of all things, ultimately including you—and your path is defined by the extent to which you strive against those aims.

#### Level 3: Dark One's Blessing

When you reduce an enemy to 0 Hit Points, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Charisma modifier plus your Warlock level (minimum of 1 Temporary Hit Point). You also gain this benefit if someone else reduces an enemy within 10 feet of you to 0 Hit Points.

#### Level 3: Fiend Spells

The magic of your patron ensures you always have certain spells ready; when you reach a Warlock level specified in the Fiend Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Fiend Spells**

| Warlock Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Burning Hands\|Burning Hands]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Command\|Command]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Scorching Ray\|Scorching Ray]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Suggestion\|Suggestion]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Fireball\|Fireball]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Stinking Cloud\|Stinking Cloud]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Fire Shield\|Fire Shield]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Wall of Fire\|Wall of Fire]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Geas\|Geas]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Insect Plague\|Insect Plague]] |

#### Level 6: Dark One's Own Luck

You can call on your fiendish patron to alter fate in your favor. When you make an ability check or a saving throw, you can use this feature to add 1d10 to your roll. You can do so after seeing the roll but before any of the roll's effects occur.

You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of once), but you can use it no more than once per roll. You regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 10: Fiendish Resilience

Choose one damage type, other than Force, whenever you finish a Short or Long Rest. You have Resistance to that damage type until you choose a different one with this feature.

#### Level 14: Hurl Through Hell

Once per turn when you hit a creature with an attack roll, you can try to instantly transport the target through the Lower Planes. The target must succeed on a Charisma saving throw against your spell save DC, or the target disappears and hurtles through a nightmare landscape. The target takes 8d10 Psychic damage if it isn't a Fiend, and it has the Incapacitated condition until the end of your next turn, when it returns to the space it previously occupied or the nearest unoccupied space.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest unless you expend a Pact Magic spell slot (no action required) to restore your use of it.

### Warlock Subclass: Great Old One Patron

_Touch the Alien Mind of a Cosmic Horror_

Your patron is an incomprehensible entity whose nature is utterly alien to the fabric of reality. Its influence grants you telepathy, psychic power, and the ability to bind other minds to your will.

#### Level 3: Awakened Mind

As a Bonus Action, you choose one creature you can see within 30 feet of yourself. You and that creature can communicate telepathically with each other for a number of minutes equal to your Warlock level, as long as you're within a number of miles of each other equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1 mile); you and the creature don't need to share a language to understand one another this way. This effect ends early if you form a new telepathic connection with this feature.

#### Level 3: Great Old One Spells

When you reach a Warlock level specified in the Great Old One Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Great Old One Spells**

| Warlock Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Detect Thoughts\|Detect Thoughts]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Dissonant Whispers\|Dissonant Whispers]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Phantasmal Force\|Phantasmal Force]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hideous Laughter\|Tasha's Hideous Laughter]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Clairvoyance\|Clairvoyance]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hunger of Hadar\|Hunger of Hadar]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Confusion\|Confusion]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Aberration\|Summon Aberration]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Modify Memory\|Modify Memory]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Telekinesis\|Telekinesis]] |

#### Level 3: Psychic Spells

When you cast a Warlock spell that deals damage, you can change its damage type to Psychic. In addition, you can cast Warlock spells from the Enchantment school or the Illusion school without Verbal or Somatic components.

#### Level 6: Clairvoyant Combatant

You can use your Awakened Mind to form a telepathic bond with a creature and force it to make a Wisdom saving throw against your spell save DC. On a failed save, for the duration of the bond, that creature has Disadvantage on attack rolls against you, and you have Advantage on attack rolls against it.

Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest, unless you expend a Pact Magic spell slot (no action required) to use it again.

#### Level 10: Eldritch Hex

You always have the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hex|Hex]] spell prepared, and it doesn't count against the number of spells you can have prepared. When you cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hex|Hex]] and choose an ability score, the target also has Disadvantage on saving throws using the chosen ability for the spell's duration.

#### Level 10: Thought Shield

Your thoughts can't be read by telepathy or other means unless you allow it, and you have Resistance to Psychic damage. Whenever a creature deals Psychic damage to you, that creature takes the same amount of Psychic damage that you take.

#### Level 14: Create Thrall

You can cast [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Aberration|Summon Aberration]] without expending a spell slot, without requiring Concentration, and its duration becomes 1 minute rather than 1 hour when cast this way. The summoned aberration gains Temporary Hit Points equal to your Warlock level plus your Charisma modifier. When the summoned aberration hits a creature affected by your [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hex|Hex]] spell, that creature takes extra Psychic damage equal to [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Hex|Hex]]'s bonus damage.

Once you use this feature in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend a spell slot of level 1 or higher (no action required) to use it again.

### Warlock Subclass: Hexblade

_Expansion subclass — Xanathar's Guide to Everything (2017). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Warlock subclasses (Archfey/Celestial/Fiend/Great Old One)—kept here as bonus content. (2024 phrasing note: the original 2014-era subclass granted its first two features at Warlock level 1; this vault's 2024-style Warlock doesn't choose a subclass until level 3, so both are presented below as Level 3, matching how the core 2024 subclasses are structured. The other legacy Warlock and Wizard subclasses below follow the same convention without repeating this note.)_

_Bind Your Pact to a Sentient, Cursed Weapon_

Your patron is a mysterious, malevolent power from the Shadowfell, one that grants you supernatural skill with a weapon and the ability to curse your foes.

#### Level 3: Hex Warrior

You gain proficiency with Medium armor, Shields, and Martial weapons. When you finish a Long Rest, you can touch one weapon that you're proficient with, provided it isn't a Two-Handed weapon unless it has the Versatile property; you gain proficiency with it if you lack it, and you can use your Charisma modifier, instead of Strength or Dexterity, for that weapon's attack and damage rolls while you wield it. If you have a weapon bonded to you through your Pact of the Blade feature, this benefit applies to it automatically.

#### Level 3: Hexblade's Curse

As a Bonus Action, you curse a creature you can see within 30 feet of yourself for 1 minute; this effect ends early if the creature dies, if you die, or if you have the Incapacitated condition. While the creature is cursed, you add your Proficiency Bonus to damage rolls you make against it, your attacks against it score a Critical Hit on a roll of 19 or 20, and if it dies while cursed, you regain Hit Points equal to your Warlock level plus your Charisma modifier. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

#### Level 6: Accursed Specter

When you slay a Humanoid, you can animate its corpse as a specter that fights for you until the end of your next Long Rest. It has Temporary Hit Points equal to half your Warlock level, and you add your Charisma modifier to its attack rolls. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest, unless you expend a spell slot of level 1 or higher (no action required) to use it again.

#### Level 10: Armor of Hexes

If the creature cursed by your Hexblade's Curse hits you with an attack roll, you can take a Reaction to roll a d6; on a roll of 4 or higher, the attack instead misses you regardless of its roll.

#### Level 14: Master of Hexes

When the creature cursed by your Hexblade's Curse dies, you can take a Bonus Action, provided you don't have the Incapacitated condition, to apply the curse to a different creature you can see within 30 feet of yourself, without regaining the Hit Points normally granted by the original target's death.

### Warlock Subclass: Fathomless

_Expansion subclass — Tasha's Cauldron of Everything (2020). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Warlock subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Bind Your Pact to the Deepest Ocean_

Your patron is an ancient, unknowable entity from the darkest depths of the ocean, granting you command over cold water and spectral tentacles.

#### Level 3: Fathomless Spells

You always have certain spells prepared. When you reach a Warlock level specified in the Fathomless Spells table, you thereafter always have the listed spells prepared.

**Fathomless Spells**

| Warlock Level | Spells |
| --- | --- |
| 3 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Create or Destroy Water\|Create or Destroy Water]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Thunderwave\|Thunderwave]] |
| 5 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Gust of Wind\|Gust of Wind]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Silence\|Silence]] |
| 7 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Lightning Bolt\|Lightning Bolt]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Sleet Storm\|Sleet Storm]] |
| 9 | [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Control Water\|Control Water]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Summon Elemental\|Summon Elemental]] (water elemental only) |

#### Level 3: Tentacle of the Deep

As a Bonus Action, you magically create a 10-foot spectral tentacle at a point you can see within 60 feet of yourself. It lasts for 1 minute, requires no Concentration, and can be dismissed early (no action required). When you create it, and again as a Bonus Action on later turns, you can make a melee spell attack against a creature within 10 feet of the tentacle; on a hit, the target takes 1d8 Cold damage (2d8 starting at level 10), and its Speed is reduced by 10 feet until the start of your next turn. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 3: Gift of the Sea

You have a Swim Speed of 40 feet, and you can breathe underwater.

#### Level 6: Guardian Coil

While your Tentacle of the Deep is present, when you or a creature you can see within 10 feet of it takes damage, you can take a Reaction to have the tentacle intervene, reducing that damage by 1d8 (2d8 starting at level 10).

#### Level 6: Oceanic Soul

You have Resistance to Cold damage. In addition, you and any creature that is fully submerged in water can understand each other's speech while you're both submerged.

#### Level 10: Grasping Tentacles

You learn the [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Black Tentacles|Evard's Black Tentacles]] spell, which doesn't count against your spells known. You can cast it once without expending a spell slot, and you regain the ability to cast it this way when you finish a Long Rest. Whenever you cast it by any means, you gain Temporary Hit Points equal to your Warlock level.

#### Level 14: Fathomless Plunge

As an action, you teleport yourself and up to five willing creatures you can see within 30 feet of yourself, reappearing up to 1 mile away in a body of water you've seen that's at least pond-sized, or within 30 feet of it. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Short or Long Rest.

### Warlock Subclass: Genie

_Expansion subclass — Tasha's Cauldron of Everything (2020). Not one of the four core 2024 PHB Warlock subclasses—kept here as bonus content._

_Bind Your Pact to a Genie_

Your patron is a genie of one of the four elemental kinds—dao, djinni, efreeti, or marid—and it has bound you to serve as its vessel-bearer in exchange for a measure of its power.

#### Level 3: Genie's Vessel

Your patron gifts you a Tiny magical object—an oil lamp, a jug, a ring, a bottle, a statuette, or a lantern—that serves as a Spellcasting Focus for your Warlock spells. The vessel has Armor Class equal to your spell save DC, Hit Points equal to your Warlock level plus your Proficiency Bonus, and Immunity to Poison and Psychic damage; it can't be damaged while you're carrying it.

_Bottled Respite._ As an action, you can vanish and enter your vessel, leaving it behind in the space you left. Inside is an extradimensional space 20 feet in radius and 20 feet high, decorated to match your genie kind. You can remain there for up to a number of hours equal to twice your Proficiency Bonus, exiting early as a Bonus Action; you also exit automatically, reappearing in the nearest unoccupied space, if you die or if the vessel is destroyed. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a Long Rest.

_Genie's Wrath._ Once on each of your turns when you hit with an attack, you can deal extra damage to the target equal to your Proficiency Bonus. The damage type depends on your genie kind: Bludgeoning (Dao), Thunder (Djinni), Fire (Efreeti), or Cold (Marid).

When you choose this subclass, choose your genie kind—Dao, Djinni, Efreeti, or Marid—which determines your Genie's Wrath damage type, your Elemental Gift Resistance, and the spells you always have prepared.

**Genie Spells**

| Warlock Level | Shared Spell | Dao Spell | Djinni Spell | Efreeti Spell | Marid Spell |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 3 | Detect Evil and Good | Sanctuary | Thunderwave | Burning Hands | Fog Cloud |
| 5 | Phantasmal Force | Spike Growth | Gust of Wind | Scorching Ray | Blur |
| 7 | Create Food and Water | Meld into Stone | Wind Wall | Fireball | Sleet Storm |
| 9 | Phantasmal Killer | Stone Shape | Greater Invisibility | Fire Shield | Control Water |
| 11 | Creation | Wall of Stone | Seeming | Flame Strike | Cone of Cold |
| 17 | Wish | Wish | Wish | Wish | Wish |

#### Level 6: Elemental Gift

You have Resistance to the damage type associated with your genie kind (as with Genie's Wrath). As a Bonus Action, you can grant yourself a Fly Speed of 30 feet, including the ability to hover, for 10 minutes. You can use this benefit a number of times equal to your Proficiency Bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 10: Sanctuary Vessel

When you use your Bottled Respite, you can bring up to five willing creatures within 30 feet of yourself inside with you; you can eject any of them individually as a Bonus Action, and all are ejected if you leave, die, or the vessel is destroyed. Any creature that remains inside for at least 10 minutes gains the benefit of a Short Rest, and while inside, creatures add your Proficiency Bonus to any Hit Points they recover by spending Hit Dice.

#### Level 14: Limited Wish

As an action, you speak your desire to your vessel, duplicating the effect of any spell of level 6 or lower that has a casting time of an action, without needing that spell's components. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish 1d4 Long Rests.

## Wizard

**Core Wizard Traits**

| Col 1 | Col 2 |
| --- | --- |
| Primary Ability | Intelligence |
| Hit Point Die | D6 per Wizard level |
| Saving Throw Proficiencies | Intelligence and Wisdom |
| Skill Proficiencies | Choose 2: Arcana, History, Insight, Investigation, Medicine, Nature, or Religion |
| Weapon Proficiencies | Simple weapons |
| Armor Training | None |
| Starting Equipment | Choose A or B: (A) 2 Daggers, Arcane Focus (Quarterstaff), Robe, Spellbook, Scholar's Pack, and 5 GP; or (B) 55 GP |

### Becoming a Wizard …

#### As a Level 1 Character

• Gain all the traits in the Core Wizard Traits table.
• Gain the Wizard's level 1 features, which are listed in the Wizard Features table.

#### As a Multiclass Character

• Gain the Hit Point Die from the Core Wizard Traits table.
• Gain the Wizard's level 1 features, which are listed in the Wizard Features table. See the multiclassing rules in "Character Creation" to determine your available spell slots.

### Wizard Class Features

As a Wizard, you gain the following class features when you reach the specified Wizard levels. These features are listed in the Wizard Features table.

**Wizard Features**

#### Level 1: Spellcasting

As a student of arcane magic, you have learned to cast spells. See "Spells" for the rules on spellcasting. The information below details how you use those rules with Wizard spells, which appear in the Wizard spell list later in the class's description.

_Cantrips._ You know three Wizard cantrips of your choice. [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Light|Light]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Hand|Mage Hand]], and [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Ray of Frost|Ray of Frost]] are recommended. Whenever you finish a Long Rest, you can replace one of your cantrips from this feature with another Wizard cantrip of your choice.

When you reach Wizard levels 4 and 10, you learn another Wizard cantrip of your choice, as shown in the Cantrips column of the Wizard Features table.

_Spellbook._ Your wizardly apprenticeship culminated in the creation of a unique book: your spellbook. It is a Tiny object that weighs 3 pounds, contains 100 pages, and can be read only by you or someone casting [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Identify|Identify]]. You determine the book's appearance and materials, such as a gilt-edged tome or a collection of vellum bound with twine.

The book contains the level 1+ spells you know. It starts with six level 1 Wizard spells of your choice. [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Detect Magic|Detect Magic]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Feather Fall|Feather Fall]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Mage Armor|Mage Armor]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Magic Missile|Magic Missile]], [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Sleep|Sleep]], and [[z_5.5e Compendium/Spells#Thunderwave|Thunderwave]] are recommended.

Whenever you gain a Wizard level after 1, add two Wizard spells of your choice to your spellbook. Each of these spells must be of a level for which you have spell slots, as shown in the Wizard Features table. The spells are the culmination of arcane research you do regularly.

_Spell Slots._ The Wizard Features table shows how many spell slots you have to cast your level 1+ spells. You regain all expended slots when you finish a Long Rest.

_Prepared Spells of Level 1+._ You prepare the list of level 1+ spells that are available for you to cast with this feature. To do so, choose four spells from your spellbook. The chosen spells must be of a level for which you have spell slots.

The number of spells on your list increases as you gain Wizard levels, as shown in the Prepared Spells column of the Wizard Features table. Whenever that number increases, choose additional Wizard spells until the number of spells on your list matches the number in the table. The chosen spells must be of a level for which you have spell slots. For example, if you're a level 3 Wizard, your list of prepared spells can include six spells of levels 1 and 2 in any combination, chosen from your spellbook.

If another Wizard feature gives you spells that you always have prepared, those spells don't count against the number of spells you can prepare with this feature, but those spells otherwise count as Wizard spells for you.

_Changing Your Prepared Spells._ Whenever you finish a Long Rest, you can change your list of prepared spells, replacing any of the spells there with spells from your spellbook.

_Spellcasting Ability._ Intelligence is your spellcasting ability for your Wizard spells.

_Spellcasting Focus._ You can use an Arcane Focus or your spellbook as a Spellcasting Focus for your Wizard spells.

#### Level 1: Ritual Adept

You can cast any spell as a Ritual if that spell has the Ritual tag and the spell is in your spellbook. You needn't have the spell prepared, but you must read from the book to cast a spell in this way.

#### Level 1: Arcane Recovery

You can regain some of your magical energy by studying your spellbook. When you finish a Short Rest, you can choose expended spell slots to recover. The spell slots can have a combined level equal to no more than half your Wizard level (round up), and none of the slots can be level 6 or higher. For example, if you're a level 4 Wizard, you can recover up to two levels' worth of spell slots, regaining either one level 2 spell slot or two level 1 spell slots.

Once you use this feature, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest.

#### Level 2: Scholar

While studying magic, you also specialized in another field of study. Choose one of the following skills in which you have proficiency: Arcana, History, Investigation, Medicine, Nature, or Religion. You have Expertise in the chosen skill.

#### Level 3: Wizard Subclass

You gain a Wizard subclass of your choice. The Evoker subclass is detailed after this class's description. A subclass is a specialization that grants you features at certain Wizard levels. For the rest of your career, you gain each of your subclass's features that are of your Wizard level or lower.

> **Expanding and Replacing a Spellbook**
>
> The spells you add to your spellbook as you gain levels reflect your ongoing magical research, but you might find other spells during your adventures that you can add to the book. You could discover a Wizard spell on a [[z_5.5e Compendium/Magic Items#Spell Scroll|Spell Scroll]], for example, and then copy it into your spellbook.
>
> _Copying a Spell into the Book._ When you find a level 1+ Wizard spell, you can copy it into your spellbook if it's of a level you can prepare and if you have time to copy it. For each level of the spell, the transcription takes 2 hours and costs 50 GP. Afterward you can prepare the spell like the other spells in your spellbook.
>
> _Copying the Book._ You can copy a spell from your spellbook into another book. This is like copying a new spell into your spellbook but faster, since you already know how to cast the spell. You need spend only 1 hour and 10 GP for each level of the copied spell.
>
> If you lose your spellbook, you can use the same procedure to transcribe the Wizard spells that you have prepared into a new spellbook. Filling out the remainder of the new book requires you to find new spells to do so. For this reason, many wizards keep a backup spellbook.

#### Level 4: Ability Score Improvement

You gain the Ability Score Improvement feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. You gain this feature again at Wizard levels 8, 12, and 16.

#### Level 5: Memorize Spell

Whenever you finish a Short Rest, you can study your spellbook and replace one of the level 1+ Wizard spells you have prepared for your Spellcasting feature with another level 1+ spell from the book.

#### Level 18: Spell Mastery

You have achieved such mastery over certain spells that you can cast them at will. Choose a level 1 and a level 2 spell in your spellbook that have a casting time of an action. You always have those spells prepared, and you can cast them at their lowest level without expending a spell slot. To cast either spell at a higher level, you must expend a spell slot.

Whenever you finish a Long Rest, you can study your spellbook and replace one of those spells with an eligible spell of the same level from the book.

#### Level 19: Epic Boon

You gain an Epic Boon feat (see "Feats") or another feat of your choice for which you qualify. Boon of Spell Recall is recommended.

#### Level 20: Signature Spells

Choose two level 3 spells in your spellbook as your signature spells. You always have these spells prepared, and you can cast each of them once at level 3 without expending a spell slot. When you do so, you can't cast them in this way again until you finish a Short or Long Rest. To cast either spell at a higher level, you must expend a spell slot.
